Collectively, these results show that a lightweight, open, harness-agnostic environment layer enables reusable agentic data, training recipes, and evaluations across domains.

</details>


### [105] [Case-Based Calibration of Adaptive Reasoning and Execution for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15041)
*Renning Pang,Tian Lan,Leyuan Liu,Piao Tong,Sheng Cao,Xiaosong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAST框架，利用历史执行轨迹作为结构化案例，指导大模型实现校准的工具使用，在提升准确率的同时显著减少了推理长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用扩展了大语言模型的能力边界，但可靠的执行需要在适当的推理深度与严格的结构有效性之间取得平衡。现有方法难以根据任务复杂度动态调整推理策略，导致不必要的计算开销或结构性错误。

Method: 提出CAST（Case-driven framework）框架，从案例驱动视角出发：将历史执行轨迹视为结构化案例，提取案例信号识别复杂度画像以估计最优推理策略，同时提取失败画像映射可能的结构性崩溃。将这些知识转化为细粒度奖励设计和自适应推理机制，使模型在强化学习过程中自主内化基于案例的策略。

Result: 在BFCLv2和ToolBench基准上，CAST实现了最高5.85个百分点的整体执行准确率提升，同时将平均推理长度减少26%，显著缓解了高影响的结构性错误，在不必要的深思熟虑与执行成功率之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了历史执行案例可为校准工具使用提供可复用的适应知识，为如何在推理深度与结构有效性之间实现动态平衡提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Tool use extends large language models beyond parametric knowledge, but reliable execution requires balancing appropriate reasoning depth with strict structural validity. We approach this problem from a case-based perspective to present CAST, a case-driven framework that treats historical execution trajectories as structured cases. Instead of reusing raw exemplar outputs, CAST extracts case-derived signals to identify complexity profiles for estimating optimal reasoning strategies, alongside failure profiles to map likely structural breakdowns. The framework translates this knowledge into a fine-grained reward design and adaptive reasoning, enabling the model to autonomously internalize case-based strategies during reinforcement learning. Experiments on BFCLv2 and ToolBench demonstrate that CAST improves both schema-faithful execution and task-level tool-use success while reducing unnecessary deliberation. The approach achieves up to 5.85 percentage points gain in overall execution accuracy and reduces average reasoning length by 26%, significantly mitigating high-impact structural errors. Ultimately, this demonstrates how historical execution cases can provide reusable adaptation knowledge for calibrated tool use.

</details>


### [106] [Dual-Dimensional Consistency: Balancing Budget and Quality in Adaptive Inference-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15100)
*Rongman Xu,Yifei Li,Tianzhe Zhao,Yanrui Wu,Bo Li,Hang Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DDC统一框架，通过结合置信度加权贝叶斯协议和趋势感知分层剪枝，协调采样宽度与深度，在保持或超越基线准确率的同时，将token消耗降低10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理时扩展方法将采样宽度（width）和深度（depth）视为正交目标，导致效率低下：宽度共识方法可能强化幻觉，而深度剪枝机制会过早截断复杂但有效的推理链。

Method: 提出Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC) 统一框架，将路径质量与自适应终止相结合。具体包括：1) Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol（置信度加权贝叶斯协议）；2) Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning（趋势感知分层剪枝），确保计算资源集中于高质量推理路径，过滤幻觉并加速共识达成。

Result: 在五个基准测试上的评估表明，该方法在各类LLM上将token消耗减少超过10倍，同时保持或超越强基线的准确率。

Conclusion: DDC框架成功解决了推理时扩展中采样预算与推理质量的权衡问题，通过统一处理宽度和深度维度，实现了高效且高质量的LLM推理。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable abilities in reasoning. However, maximizing their potential through inference-time scaling faces challenges in trade-off between sampling budget and reasoning quality. Current strategies remain inefficient as they typically treat sampling width and depth as orthogonal objectives, where width consensus methods risk reinforcing hallucinations, while depth pruning mechanisms prematurely truncate complex yet valid reasoning chains. Therefore, we propose Dual-Dimensional Consistency (DDC), a unified framework that bridges path quality with adaptive termination. By coupling Confidence-Weighted Bayesian protocol with a Trend-Aware Stratified Pruning, our method ensures that computational resources are concentrated on high quality reasoning paths, filtering hallucinations while accelerating consensus. Evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that this approach reduces token consumption by over 10 times while maintaining or exceeding the accuracy of strong baselines across various LLMs.

</details>


### [107] [Why Neighborhoods Matter: Traversal Context and Provenance in Agentic GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15109)
*Riccardo Terrenzi,Maximilian von Zastrow,Serkan Ayvaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在Agentic GraphRAG系统中，引用不仅是支持答案的证据，还需考虑图遍历过程、结构和未引用实体的影响；引用评估应从单一来源支持转向更广泛的检索轨迹溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic GraphRAG系统中的智能体会先探索知识图谱再生成答案和引用，这使得引用的忠实性变得复杂。现有评估方法难以捕捉图遍历过程和未引用上下文对答案的影响，因此需要重新定义引用忠实性。

Method: 将引用忠实性定义为轨迹级别问题，通过控制消融实验，分别对比隔离、移除和遮蔽已引用和未引用图实体的效果，分析其对最终答案的影响程度。

Result: 实验发现：引用证据通常是必要的，移除它会显著改变答案并降低准确性；但引用并不充分，准确答案也可能依赖于未引用的遍历上下文和周围的图结构。

Conclusion: Agentic GraphRAG中的引用评估应超越传统的来源支持范式，转向对更广泛检索轨迹的溯源考量，重视遍历过程和图结构对答案生成的综合影响。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation can improve factuality by grounding answers in external evidence, but Agentic GraphRAG complicates what it means for citations to be faithful. In these systems, an agent explores a knowledge graph before producing an answer and a small set of citations. We frame citation faithfulness as a trajectory-level problem: final citations should not only support the answer, but also account for the graph traversal, structure, and visited-but-uncited entities that may influence it. Through controlled ablation experiments, we compare the effects of isolating, removing, and masking cited and uncited graph entities. Our results show that cited evidence is often necessary, as removing it substantially changes answers and reduces accuracy. However, citations are not sufficient, because accurate answers can also depend on uncited traversal context and surrounding graph structure. These findings suggest that citation evaluation in Agentic GraphRAG should move beyond source support toward provenance over the broader retrieval trajectory.

</details>


### [108] [OpenDeepThink: Parallel Reasoning via Bradley--Terry Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15177)
*Shang Zhou,Wenhao Chai,Kaiyuan Liu,Huanzhi Mao,Qiuyang Mang,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenDeepThink框架，通过Bradley-Terry成对比较实现测试时计算的广度扩展，在Codeforces上将Gemini 3.1 Pro的有效Elo提升405分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时计算方法主要通过扩展单一推理路径的深度来提升性能。而通过并行采样多个候选的广度扩展方法面临选择瓶颈：在没有真实验证器的情况下，点对点LLM评判存在噪声和偏差，难以准确选择最佳候选。

Method: 提出OpenDeepThink，一种基于群体的测试时计算框架。每轮迭代中：(1) LLM对随机候选对进行比较评判；(2) 通过Bradley-Terry模型将投票聚合成全局排名；(3) 保留排名靠前的候选，前3/4使用比较过程中生成的自然语言评论进行变异，后1/4被丢弃。通过8轮顺序LLM调用实现迭代优化。

Result: (1) 在Codeforces上将Gemini 3.1 Pro的有效Elo提升+405分（约27分钟墙钟时间）；(2) 框架可在无需重新调参的情况下迁移至更弱或更强的模型；(3) 在多领域HLE基准测试中，增益集中在客观可验证领域，而在主观领域出现反向效果；(4) 发布CF-73数据集，包含73个专家评级（国际特级大师标注）的Codeforces问题，与官方判决的本地评估一致性达99%。

Conclusion: OpenDeepThink通过Bradley-Terry成对比较有效解决了测试时广度扩展中的选择瓶颈问题，显著提升了LLM在竞争性编程任务上的表现，且具有良好的跨模型迁移能力。该框架在客观可验证领域表现优异，为测试时计算扩展提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Test-time compute scaling is a primary axis for improving LLM reasoning. Existing methods primarily scale depth by extending a single reasoning trace. Scaling breadth by sampling multiple candidates in parallel is straightforward, but introduces a selection bottleneck: choosing the best candidate without a ground-truth verifier, since pointwise LLM judging is noisy and biased. To address this, we introduce OpenDeepThink, a population-based test-time compute framework that selects via pairwise Bradley-Terry comparison. Each generation, the LLM judges random pairs of candidates and aggregates votes via Bradley-Terry into a global ranking; top-ranked candidates are preserved and the top three quarters are mutated using the natural-language critiques produced during comparison; the bottom quarter is discarded. OpenDeepThink raises Gemini 3.1 Pro's effective Codeforces Elo by +405 points in eight sequential LLM-call rounds (~27 minutes wall-clock). The pipeline transfers across weaker and stronger models without retuning, and on the multi-domain HLE benchmark, gains appear concentrated in objectively verifiable domains and reverse in subjective ones. We release CF-73, a curated set of 73 expert-rated Codeforces problems with International Grandmaster annotation and 99% local-evaluation agreement against the official verdict.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Distributed Statistical Zero-Knowledge Proofs via Sumcheck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14015)
*Benjamin Jauregui,Masayuki Miyamoto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个分布式统计零知识Sumcheck协议，并将其应用于非k-可着色性和子图计数问题，同时证明了对特定问题的轮数压缩下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式交互证明领域发展迅速，但现有的分布式零知识证明技术非常有限且多为特定问题设计，缺乏通用技术。本文旨在填补这一空白，引入分布式统计零知识概念，并将经典的Sumcheck协议提升为分布式零知识证明的模块化原语。

Method: 核心方法是设计分布式零知识Sumcheck协议。给定对有限域𝔽上N变量多项式F的预言机访问，协议在O(N)轮内使用O(log|𝔽|)比特消息验证形如∑F(x)=a的声明，同时实现统计零知识和低可靠性错误。作者将该原语应用于非k-可着色性问题（任意常数k）和子图计数问题（给定k节点模式的计数）。

Result: 对非k-可着色性问题，获得了O(n)轮、O(log^{1+o(1)}n)比特消息的分布式统计零知识证明，这是该问题的首个非平凡分布式交互证明（即使没有零知识保证）。对子图计数问题，获得了O(k log n)轮、O(k log n)比特的分布式统计零知识证明，改进了之前的分布式交互证明并额外提供统计零知识。此外，证明了Sumcheck的额外轮数压缩具有问题依赖性：对于常数度图上的非3-可着色性，在多项式时间本地计算下排除了o(n/log n)轮的可能性。

Conclusion: 本文成功将经典Sumcheck协议适配到分布式零知识场景，为分布式零知识证明提供了首个通用原语，在两个经典问题上取得了突破性成果，同时建立了轮数压缩的理论下界，为该领域后续研究奠定了重要基础。

Abstract: We study distributed zero-knowledge proofs, introduced by Bick, Kol, and Oshman (SODA 2022). While distributed interactive proofs have advanced rapidly, general-purpose techniques for distributed zero-knowledge remain limited and mostly problem-specific. We address this gap by introducing distributed statistical zero-knowledge, requiring that each node's view be simulatable within negligible statistical distance, and by lifting the classical Sumcheck protocol (Lund, Fortnow, Karloff, and Nisan, FOCS 1990) into a modular primitive for distributed zero-knowledge proofs.
  Our main contribution is a distributed zero-knowledge implementation of Sumcheck. Given oracle access to a polynomial F over a finite field $\mathbb{F}$ with N variables, we design a protocol verifying claims of the form $\sum_{x\in\mathbb{F}} F(x)=a$ using $O(N)$ rounds of $O(\log |\mathbb{F}|)$-bit messages, while achieving statistical zero-knowledge and small soundness error.
  We apply this primitive to two problems. For non-k-colorability, we obtain an $O(n)$-round distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof deciding whether a graph is not k-colorable, for any constant k, using $O(log^{1+o(1)} n)$-bit messages. This is the first nontrivial distributed interactive proof for this problem, even without zero-knowledge guarantees. For Subgraph Counting, we obtain an $O(k \log n)$-round, $O(k \log n)$-bit distributed statistical zero-knowledge proof for counting copies of a given k-node pattern, improving previous distributed interactive proofs while additionally providing statistical zero-knowledge. Finally, we show that additional round compression of Sumcheck is problem-dependent: for non-3-colorability on constant-degree graphs, we prove a lower bound excluding $o(n/\log n)$ rounds under polynomial-time local computation.

</details>


### [110] [Semi-Synchronous Exploration in Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14375)
*Ashish Saxena,Anisur Rahaman Molla,Kaushik Mondal,Gokarna Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了半同步调度下1-区间连通动态图中的图探索问题，确定了对抗者每轮可停用代理数量的紧阈值⌈k/(n-2)⌉-1，并给出了匹配该界限的探索算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在动态图环境下，当对抗者可在每轮停用部分代理时，移动代理完成图探索任务的可行性及所需条件，揭示代理数量、能力与对抗强度之间的精确权衡关系。

Method: 采用1-区间连通动态图模型（每轮拓扑可变但保持连通），在半同步调度下，通过构建不可行性证明确定对抗者停用代理数量的上界，并设计匹配该界限的探索算法，分析代理所需的最小能力（1跳可见性与1跳通信）。

Result: 证明了当对抗者每轮可停用至少⌈k/(n-2)⌉-1个代理时，探索不可能完成；由此推出探索可行的条件为对抗者每轮至多停用⌈k/(n-2)⌉-2个代理；在该阈值处，代理必须具备1跳可见性和1跳通信能力；提出了在对抗者每轮停用至多⌈k/(n-2)⌉-2个代理、代理具备1跳可见性和全局通信条件下的探索算法。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了1-区间连通动态图中半同步调度下代理探索问题的精确界限，揭示了对抗者停用能力与代理数量之间的定量关系，并证明了在临界条件下代理必须具备的最小能力要求，为动态环境中的分布式探索提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the fundamental problem of graph exploration in dynamic graphs using mobile agents. We consider $1$-interval connected dynamic graphs, where the topology may change arbitrarily from round to round as long as the graph remains connected, and edges are assigned with the dynamic port labeling at each round. The execution follows a semi-synchronous scheduler, under which an adversary may deactivate an arbitrary subset of agents in each round. For a graph with $n$ nodes and $k$ agents, we show that exploration is impossible if the adversary can deactivate at least $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 1$ agents per round, even when agents are equipped with unbounded memory, have global communication and full visibility. This yields an upper bound, implying that exploration is solvable only when the adversary deactivates at most $\left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents per round. We further establish that achieving exploration at this threshold requires agents to have both $1$-hop visibility and $1$-hop communication. Finally, we present the exploration algorithm using $k$ agents when the adversary deactivates at most $ \left\lceil \frac{k}{n-2} \right\rceil - 2$ agents, assuming agents are equipped with $1$-hop visibility and global communication, and matches the adversarial deactivation bound implied by the impossibility results.

</details>


### [111] [Fast Gossip-based Rumor Spreading using Small Messages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14376)
*Fabien Dufoulon,William K. Moses,Gopal Pandurangan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了在仅使用多对数级（polylog n）消息大小的条件下，仍可实现快速谣言传播，提出了两种分别基于弱电导率和网络直径的近最优算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有快速Gossip算法虽然每轮仅联系一个邻居，但通常依赖线性于网络规模n的大消息，这削弱了Gossip通信轻量化的优势。本文旨在解决一个基础问题：能否仅使用小消息实现快速Gossip谣言传播。

Method: 核心创新是以一种新颖的方式利用图草图（graph sketches）技术。通过该技术克服通信瓶颈，在保持消息大小为多对数级（polylog n）的同时，实现对图结构信息的高效聚合与传播。

Result: 提出了两种算法：1. 基于弱电导率Φ_c的算法，时间复杂度为O(c log n / Φ_c)，该界限本质上是最优的；2. 基于网络直径D的算法，高概率在Õ(D+√n)轮内完成，且可改造用于构建最小生成树（MST），该复杂度即使在非Gossip算法中也是近最优的。

Conclusion: 本文肯定地回答了“能否使用小消息进行快速Gossip”这一问题，证明仅使用多对数级消息大小即可达到近最优的时间复杂度，显著降低了Gossip算法的通信开销。

Abstract: We study gossip algorithms for the fundamental rumor spreading problem, where the goal is to disseminate a rumor from a given source node to all nodes in an arbitrary (and unknown) graph. Gossip algorithms allow each node to call only one neighbor per round and are therefore highly message-efficient, with low per-node communication overhead per round. The state of the art present fast gossip algorithms, however they typically leverage large-sized messages. This undermines the light-weight communication advantage of gossip, since even though only one neighbor is contacted per round, the message size can be linear in $n$, the network size. Hence, a fundamental question is whether one can perform fast gossip using small messages.
  The main contribution of this paper is to answer the above question in the affirmative and present two gossip algorithms that achieve fast rumor spreading using messages of polylog{n} size. Specifically, we present the following algorithms:
  1. An algorithm that runs in $O(c \log n / Φ_c)$ rounds for every $c \geq 1$, and $Φ_c$ is the weak conductance. Our bound in terms of weak conductance is essentially optimal.
  2. An algorithm that depends on the network diameter (and is independent of the graph's conductance), which runs in $\tilde{O}(D+\sqrt{n})$ rounds with high probability. Our algorithm can be modified to output a minimum spanning tree (MST) in the same number of rounds, which is essentially round-optimal (even for non-gossip algorithms).
  Our gossip algorithms use graph sketches [Ahn, Guha, McGregor, SODA 2012] in a novel way to overcome communication bottlenecks and achieve small communication overhead with small message sizes.

</details>


### [112] [Analysis of wireless network access logs for a hierarchical characterization of user mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14540)
*Francisco Talavera,Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Wi-Fi连接数据的分层用户移动性建模方法，通过递归分组接入点降低大规模场景复杂度，在巴利阿里群岛大学校园案例中验证了模型有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在高规模场景下降低移动性模型的复杂度，并增强具有不同地理空间特征场景之间的适应性，需要一种能够从Wi-Fi基础设施获取数据并构建分层用户移动模型的方法。

Method: 基于Wi-Fi连接数据分析，将接入点根据地理空间特征递归分组为不同粒度层级；用户轨迹定义为Wi-Fi接入点序列；采用用户画像方法结合聚类算法，每种用户类型由覆盖区域间的转移矩阵和区域停留时间向量定义；构建分层区域定义以简化模型。

Result: 在巴利阿里群岛大学校园的应用案例中，通过均方误差分析表明，所提方法的转移矩阵效果良好，但时间向量定义仍需改进；分层模型（每栋建筑一个区域，三层）相比非分层模型（整个校园一个区域，一层）复杂度更低。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效生成分层用户移动模型，在大规模场景中显著降低模型复杂度，转移矩阵建模效果良好，但时间向量建模方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: This paper presents a method that generates a hierarchical user mobility model from the analysis of the data available from Wi-Fi connections. The data obtained from the Wi-Fi infrastructure is defined in terms of the coverage areas of the access points that the users move through. These access points are recursively grouped into different levels of granularity based on their geospatial features. The track of a user is defined as a sequence of Wi-Fi access points, which is enough to simulate user mobility in, for example, fog scenarios. The hierarchical definition of the region under study is proposed to reduce the complexity of the model in high-scale scenarios and to increase the adaptability between scenarios with different geospatial features. The model creation is based on a user profiling method that uses a clustering algorithm and each user type is defined with a transition matrix between coverage areas and a time length vector for the areas. The method is applied to the case of the campus of the University of the Balearic Islands. From the analysis of the mean square error of the results, we determined that the proposed method obtains good results for the transition matrices, but that the time vector definition should be improved. The results also show lower complexity in the case of the hierarchical model, with one area for each building and three levels, in regard to a non-hierarchical model, with only one area and one level for the whole campus.

</details>


### [113] [Multi-objective application placement in fog computing using graph neural network-based reinforcement learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14649)
*Isaac Lera,Carlos Guerrero*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于深度强化学习的雾计算应用放置框架，通过图神经网络和双行动者-评论家架构实现多目标优化，在毫秒级时间内获得与遗传算法相当的帕累托解集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的雾计算应用放置优化方法（如整数线性规划、遗传算法）存在计算耗时长的问题，无法满足实时性需求。为了在保证优化效果的同时实现实时决策，研究旨在利用深度强化学习在训练后能够快速求解的优势，解决多目标应用放置优化挑战。

Method: 提出一个基于深度强化学习的框架，采用图神经网络结合两个行动者-评论家架构。该模型在学习过程中将服务间的依赖关系作为关键因素纳入位置决策考量，优先为具有更高依赖性的服务选择位置，从而实现对构成应用的互联服务优先级的全局把控。

Result: 在实验案例中，该框架生成的帕累托解集与基线策略和遗传算法结果相当，但执行时间大幅缩短至毫秒级别，而传统替代方法通常需要数小时。

Conclusion: 基于深度强化学习的雾计算应用放置框架能够在保证优化质量的同时显著提高求解速度，具备实时处理能力，为雾计算环境下的应用放置问题提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a framework designed to tackle a multi-objective optimization challenge related to the placement of applications in fog computing, employing a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach. Unlike other optimization techniques, such as integer linear programming or genetic algorithms, DRL models are applied in real time to solve similar problem situations after training. Our model comprises a learning process featuring a graph neural network and two actor-critics, providing a holistic perspective on the priorities concerning interconnected services that constitute an application. The learning model incorporates the relationships between services as a crucial factor in placement decisions: Services with higher dependencies take precedence in location selection. Our experimental investigation involves illustrative cases where we compare our results with baseline strategies and genetic algorithms. We observed a comparable Pareto set with negligible execution times, measured in the order of milliseconds, in contrast to the hours required by alternative approaches.

</details>


### [114] [Malleable Molecular Dynamics Simulations with GROMACS and DMR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14655)
*Petter Sandås,Sergio Iserte,Íñigo Aréjula-Aísa,Berk Hess,Antonio J. Peña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将动态资源管理中间件DMR集成到GROMACS分子动力学引擎中，实现了MPI进程可塑性，使作业能根据负载动态调整进程数量，从而节省计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算（HPC）中的静态资源分配在面对时变工作负载时效率低下，会导致资源闲置、队列延迟增加以及节点小时成本升高。为了解决这些问题，需要实现资源的动态管理。

Method: 将动态资源管理（DMR）中间件集成到GROMACS分子动力学引擎中。该方案结合了通信效率感知的重配置策略与GROMACS原生的检查点/重启机制，使得MPI进程数量可以根据负载动态调整，并通过Slurm调度器实现MPI进程的可塑性。

Result: 在MareNostrum 5超级计算机上进行了评估，对比了动态运行与静态执行的差异，量化了重配置开销、求解时间以及突发性GROMACS工作负载下的节点小时节省情况。

Conclusion: 通过DMR与GROMACS的集成，成功实现了MPI进程的动态调整，证明了该方案在应对突发性工作负载时，相比静态资源分配能够有效降低节点小时成本并优化求解时间。

Abstract: Static resource allocations in high-performance computing (HPC) lead to inefficiencies for time-varying workloads, causing idle resources, queue delays, and higher node-hour costs. The Dynamic Management of Resources (DMR) middleware enables MPI process malleability in Slurm via a simple API decoupled from scheduler internals. In this work, we integrate DMR into the GROMACS molecular dynamics engine to obtain a malleable variant that can dynamically adapt its MPI process count by combining communication-efficiency-aware reconfiguration with GROMACS' native checkpoint/restart mechanism. We evaluate this design on the MareNostrum~5 supercomputer, comparing dynamic runs against static executions and quantifying reconfiguration overheads, time-to-solution, and node-hour savings for bursty GROMACS workloads.

</details>


### [115] [Supervised Distributed Computing: Efficiency and Robustness under a Majority of Adversarial Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14784)
*John Augustine,Henning Hillebrandt,Manish Kumar,Christian Scheideler,Julian Werthmann*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种监督式分布式计算方案，将可对抗的工人比例容忍度从小常数提升至任意常数β<1，同时保持诚实工人的预期工作量接近单次执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的master-worker范式中，主节点需要亲自验证工人任务执行的正确性，这造成了严重的性能瓶颈。现有的监督式方案仅能应对较小的对抗工人比例。为了解决这一瓶颈并扩展对抗模型的适用范围，研究旨在为任意常数级别的对抗比例设计鲁棒且高效的方案。

Method: 采用监督式分布式计算范式，引入监督者、源节点、目标节点和工人集合。源节点存储输入，目标节点存储输出，监督者指导工人执行无环任务图中的任务。关键创新在于将任务正确性检查工作从监督者外包给工人，并假设存在轻量级验证机制允许诚实工人检查任务输出的正确性。

Result: 证明了对于任意常数β<1（即对抗工人比例可达接近100%），都存在鲁棒且高效的监督式解决方案。诚实工人的预期工作量接近每个任务单次执行，显著优于目前已知的所有鲁棒master-worker和P2P方法。

Conclusion: 该工作突破了监督式分布式计算对抗容忍度的限制，证明了在具备轻量级验证机制的前提下，即使面对高比例的对抗工人，仍能实现接近最优的工作效率，为不信任环境下的分布式计算提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We consider a recently proposed \emph{supervised distributed computing} paradigm \cite{augustine2025supervised} that extends and refines the standard master-worker paradigm for parallel computations. In this paradigm, there is a supervisor, a source, a target, and a collection of workers. The distributed computation is given as an acyclic task graph that is known to the supervisor. The source initially stores the input and the target is supposed to store the output of the computation. The individual tasks of the computation are supposed to be executed by the workers under the guidance of the supervisor. The source, target and supervisor are assumed to be reliable, while a $β$-fraction of the workers might be adversarial, for some $β\in [0,1)$. This covers, for example, the case where a supervisor has to work with untrusted volunteers. In the standard master-worker approach, the master checks whether the workers correctly execute the assigned tasks, creating a severe bottleneck, whereas in the supervised approach, the supervisor outsources this checking to the workers. Prior to this work, only supervised solutions were known for the case that $β$ is a sufficiently small constant. We show that robust and efficient supervised solutions are possible for \emph{any} constant $β<1$ while the expected work for the honest workers is close to a \emph{single} execution per task, given that there is a lightweight verification mechanism that allows honest workers to check the correctness of task outputs, which is significantly better than all robust master-worker as well as peer-to-peer approaches known so far.

</details>


### [116] [Embedded Made Easy -- Rethinking Embedded + Cloud Software Development (WIP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14863)
*Anthony Arnold,Mark Marron*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一语言和运行时系统的构想，旨在解决边缘/嵌入式应用开发中的软件栈异构性和调试复杂性问题，实现应用在边缘和云之间的无缝扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘/嵌入式应用工程化与部署面临严峻挑战：软件栈非同构、诊断与调试复杂、不同组件需使用不同语言与运行时、设计者需提前固定组件部署位置，且分布式应用在多栈环境下故障分析与调试极为困难。

Method: 提出一种统一语言与运行时系统，使应用在单一环境中完成开发与测试后可部署至系统任意组件（从资源受限控制器到大型云服务器）；该可重定向栈集成诊断与调试工具，支持开发者本地记录与回放分布式事件以进行分析与调试。

Result: 该系统允许应用在边缘与云之间无缝扩展，实现单一开发测试环境与灵活部署；集成化的诊断与调试能力使分布式事件的本地记录与回放成为可能，显著简化了分布式应用的分析与调试工作。

Conclusion: 统一语言与运行时系统为边缘/云应用开发提供了新范式，有效解决了异构性与调试难题，为未来边缘云计算环境下的应用开发与运维提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The process of engineering and deploying applications in the edge/embedded space is massively complicated by the non-homogeneous nature of the software stack and the complexity of diagnostics & debugging. Often different languages and runtimes are used for different components of the system forcing designers to, irrevocably, make decisions about what components run on the periphery and what components run in the cloud. Further complications arise when handling and diagnosing failures in the system. Multiple stacks and, often, limited support for debugging complicate the already difficult task of analyzing distributed applications.
  This paper presents a work-in-progress vision for a unified language and runtime system that allows applications to scale seamlessly across the edge and cloud. Using a single language and runtime, applications can be developed and tested in a single environment, and then deployed to any component of the system -- from resource limited controllers to large cloud servers. Further, we outline how this retargetable stack can provide integrated diagnostics and debugging tools that allow developers to record and replay distributed events locally for analysis and debugging.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [A Hardware-Aware, Per-Layer Methodology for Post-Training Quantization of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14929)
*Earl Killian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SOP的大语言模型权重量化方法，能在4.5-6比特/权重的低存储成本下实现接近无损的精度，优于传统的FP8基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大语言模型权重在低比特量化（4.5-6比特）下保持高保真度的挑战，并证明在特定硬件支持下，精心设计的块缩放小原子结构可以替代广泛部署的FP8格式，从而降低存储成本。

Method: 该方法是一种训练后量化技术，结合了多种策略：固定与动态码本对的逐层搜索、由逐块选择位控制的码本选择、带符号的逐块缩放、激活加权余弦选择、针对敏感层的多选背包提升算法，以及离群值和稀疏残差校正。硬件层面引入了高效的查找表输出格式（HIF）。

Result: 在六个开源模型家族上的实验表明，推荐的FP6工作点（6.5 bpw）在比传统逐层POT FP8基线（8.0 bpw）低1.5 bpw存储成本的情况下，实现了更低的权重重构误差。

Conclusion: 通过结合算法与硬件协同优化，SOP方法证明了块缩放小原子结构配合精确的缩放精度，可以有效替代传统的FP8量化方案，在大幅降低存储需求的同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: Scaled Outer Product (SOP) is a post-training quantization methodology for large language model weights, designed to deliver near-lossless fidelity at 4.5--6 bits per weight on hardware with per-layer LUT decode. The methodology combines per-layer search of fixed and dynamic codebook pairs selected by a per-block selection bit, signed per-block scales, activation-weighted cosine selection, and multiple-choice knapsack promotion of sensitive layers with outlier and sparse-residual correction. Fixed codebooks include NF4, BOF4, Split87, and SH4; per-layer optimized codebooks (DD4) are hosted in LUT SRAM. A new hardware-efficient LUT output format (HIF) is proposed to improve performance, energy, and cost. Across six open model families, the recommended FP6 operating point (E2M3sUE4M4, 6.5 bpw) achieves lower weight reconstruction error than the conventional per-layer-POT FP8 baseline (E4M3, 8.0 bpw) at 1.5 bpw lower storage cost, demonstrating that block-scaled small atoms with carefully chosen scale precision can replace conventionally-deployed FP8. Full evaluation across the 4.5--6 bpw range, including layer promotion and sparse residual correction, is reported in a companion paper.

</details>


### [118] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复用的ptSTL运行时监控方法，通过语义基向量实现对图像观察的安全认证，在长视野下认证边界提升4倍，并在Waymo真实驾驶数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在部分可观察条件下，如何从图像观察中进行过去时信号时序逻辑(ptSTL)的可认证运行时监控。核心挑战在于需要从图像推断安全相关量并提供有限样本保证，同时实现可复用性——即训练校准后能认证目标片段中的任意公式，而无需针对每个公式重新训练。

Method: 针对由有限时序原子字典诱导的逻辑片段，提出语义基方法——将原子鲁棒性分数向量作为预测目标，证明其是单调、1-Lipschitz可复用接口类中的最小预测目标。通过从解析树导出的确定性解码器评估公式，单次保形校准即可认证整个片段（无需联合界限）。同时提出滚动预测监控器，仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时序历史。

Result: 在行人交叉口基准测试上，滚动监控器在短视野下获得更紧的认证边界，语义基监控器在长视野下认证边界紧度提升达4倍。在Waymo真实驾驶数据验证中，两种监控器均满足保形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基监控器在可复用性和长视野性能方面表现优越，而滚动预测监控器在学习难度和短视野性能方面具有优势。该工作首次实现了基于图像观察的可复用ptSTL认证监控，为自动驾驶等安全关键系统提供了有效的安全保障方法。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [119] [Mechanistic Interpretability of EEG Foundation Models via Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13930)
*William Lehn-Schiøler,Magnus Ruud Kjær,Rahul Thapa,Magnus Guldberg Pedersen,Anton Storgaard Mosquera,Nick Williams,Radu Gatej,Tue Lehn-Schiøler,Sándor Beniczky,Sadasivan Puthusserypady,James Zou,Lars Kai Hansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE）对三种EEG基础模型进行可解释性分析，揭示了临床概念（异常、年龄、性别、药物）的单义性与纠缠特性，发现了模型表示的关键缺陷，并通过谱解码器将潜在干预转化为可解释的频谱特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型在临床任务中表现优异，但其预测的内部计算过程缺乏透明度，阻碍了临床信任。为解决此问题，需对模型内部表示进行可解释性分析，揭示其特征与临床概念之间的关联。

Method: 对三种架构不同的EEG Transformer模型（SleepFM、REVE、LaBraM）应用TopK稀疏自编码器（SAE），从嵌入中提取稀疏特征字典。基于临床分类（异常、年龄、性别、药物）基准测试单义性与纠缠性，采用概念引导（concept steering）量化引导选择性，并通过谱解码器将潜在干预映射回振幅谱。

Result: 统一的超参数流程在三种架构上稳健迁移。发现了三种操作机制：选择性可引导、已编码但纠缠、未编码。揭示了关键表示失败："破坏球"干预导致全局性能崩溃，以及临床纠缠（如年龄-病理混淆）。谱解码器成功将干预转化为生理可解释的频谱特征（如病理性慢波抑制和α波段恢复）。

Conclusion: 提出的可解释性框架系统揭示了EEG基础模型的内部表示特性，暴露了临床相关的纠缠问题，为模型透明度和临床信任提供了新的分析工具与见解。

Abstract: EEG foundation models achieve state-of-the-art clinical performance, yet the internal computations driving their predictions remain opaque: a barrier to clinical trust. We apply TopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) across three architecturally distinct EEG transformers: SleepFM, REVE, and LaBraM to extract sparse feature dictionaries from their embeddings. By grounding these features in a clinical taxonomy (abnormality, age, sex, and medication), we benchmark monosemanticity and entanglement across architectures. A single hyperparameter procedure, driven by an intrinsic dictionary health audit, transfers robustly across all three architectures. Via concept steering, we introduce a "target vs. off-target" probe area metric to quantify steering selectivity and reveal three operational regimes: selectively steerable, encoded but entangled, and non-encoded. This framework exposes critical representational failures: "wrecking-ball" interventions that collapse global model performance, and clinical entanglements, such as age-pathology confounding, where it is impossible to suppress one concept without corrupting the other. Finally, a spectral decoder maps these interventions back to the amplitude spectrum, translating latent manipulations into physiologically interpretable frequency signatures, such as pathological slow-wave suppression and $α$-band restoration.

</details>


### [120] [EMA: Efficient Model Adaptation for Learning-based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13942)
*Daiyang Yu,Xinyu Chen,Yihan Zhang,Yan Liang,Yaqi Qiao,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EMA系统，这是首个支持学习型系统适应环境变化的自适应系统。通过状态转换器减少模型训练开销，并优化数据标注成本，在8个系统上实现了14.9-42.4%的适应成本降低和6.9-31.3%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在网络化系统（如资源管理、网络仿真）中的应用日益增多，但现有学习型系统缺乏对动态环境变化的适应能力。网络化系统常运行在异构、长期且动态变化的环境中，输入条件（如网络负载）和运行目标会随时间改变，导致模型训练成本高、数据收集量大、系统性能下降和响应缓慢等问题。

Method: EMA采用系统驱动、以数据为中心的方法，通过两个关键技术解决部署挑战：（1）引入状态转换器（state transformers），将新环境的输入状态与先前的相似状态对齐，实现模型热启动适应，从而减少昂贵的模型训练；（2）通过优先标注高效用数据并平衡训练与标注成本之间的权衡，解决数据标注过程成本高昂的问题。

Result: 在8个代表性学习型系统上的评估显示，EMA将适应成本（如GPU训练时间）降低了14.9-42.4%，同时将系统性能（如网络吞吐量）提升了6.9-31.3%。

Conclusion: EMA成功解决了学习型系统在动态环境中的适应性问题，通过状态对齐和智能数据标注策略，显著降低了操作开销并提升了系统性能，为网络化系统中的ML模型部署提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) is increasingly applied to optimize system performance in tasks such as resource management and network simulation. Unlike traditional ML tasks (e.g., image classification), networked systems often operate in heterogeneous, long-running, and dynamic environment states, where input conditions (e.g., network loads) and operational objectives can shift over time and across settings. Existing learning-based systems offer little support for adaptation, resulting in costly model training, extensive data collection, degraded system performance, and slow responsiveness.
  This paper presents EMA, the first model adaptation system supporting learning-based systems to adapt to evolving environments with minimal operational overhead. EMA takes a system-driven, data-centric approach that accommodates diverse system and model designs while addressing two key deployment challenges. First, it reduces expensive model training by introducing state transformers that align the input state of a new environment with previously similar states, allowing models to warm-start adaptation. Second, it addresses the often-overlooked yet costly process of data labeling--collecting ground truth for exploring and training on various system decisions--by prioritizing labeling high-utility data while balancing the tradeoff between training and labeling cost. Evaluations on eight representative learning-based systems show that EMA reduces adaptation costs (e.g., GPU training time) by 14.9-42.4% while improving system performance (e.g., network throughput) by 6.9-31.3%.

</details>


### [121] [Rethinking Molecular OOD Generalization via Target-Aware Source Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13932)
*Zhuohao Lin,Kun Li,Jiameng Chen,Jiajun Yu,Duanhua Cao,Yizhen Zheng,Wenbin Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了SCOPE-BENCH基准和POMA框架，分别解决分子性质预测中OOD评估不准确和极端结构偏移下域适应困难的问题，显著提升了模型在极端分布外场景下的预测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的scaffold-splitting协议无法有效阻断微观语义重叠，导致模型易陷入捷径学习并高估其外推能力；同时，传统的域适应范式在极端结构偏移下表现不佳，盲目对齐异构源库会引入拓扑噪声并引发负迁移。为了解决这两个关键挑战，需要建立更严格的评估基准和更有效的适应框架。

Method: 提出了两个核心方法：1) SCOPE-BENCH基准：基于显式物理化学描述符空间中的簇级划分构建；2) POMA框架：将知识迁移建模为“检索-组合-适应”流程，首先识别结构上接近未标记目标的标记源骨架作为代理目标，然后通过强化学习策略从指数级候选池中自适应选择最优源子集，最后在宏观拓扑和微观药效团两个尺度上进行双尺度域适应。

Result: 在SCOPE-BENCH上，最先进的3D分子模型预测误差最高增加8.0倍，平均增加5.9倍；POMA框架在平均绝对误差上最高降低11.2%，在多种骨干架构上平均相对改进6.2%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了现有模型在极端OOD场景下的性能严重下降问题，证明了SCOPE-BENCH作为严格评估基准的有效性，并验证了POMA框架通过多源自适应和双尺度适应能够显著提升分子性质预测的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Robust prediction of molecular properties under extreme out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios is a pivotal bottleneck in AI-driven drug discovery. Current scaffold-splitting protocols fail to obstruct microscopic semantic overlap, predisposing models to shortcut learning and overestimating their true extrapolation capability; meanwhile, conventional domain adaptation paradigms suffer under extreme structural shifts, as blindly aligning heterogeneous source libraries injects topological noise and triggers negative transfer. To address these two challenges, scaffold-cluster out-of-distribution performance evaluation benchmark (SCOPE-BENCH), a benchmark built on cluster-level partitioning in an explicit physicochemical descriptor space, is proposed alongside policy optimization for multi-source adaptation (POMA), a framework that formulates knowledge transfer as a retrieve-compose-adapt pipeline: labeled source scaffolds structurally close to the unlabeled target are first identified as proxy targets; a reinforcement learning policy then adaptively selects the optimal source subset from an exponentially large candidate pool; and dual-scale domain adaptation is finally performed at macroscopic topological and microscopic pharmacophore scales. Evaluations show that prediction errors of state-of-the-art 3D molecular models surge by up to 8.0x on SCOPE-BENCH with a mean of 5.9x, while POMA achieves up to an 11.2% reduction in mean absolute error with an average relative improvement of 6.2% across diverse backbone architectures. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Molecular-OOD-Code-73F6.

</details>


### [122] [CSI-JEPA: Towards Foundation Representations for Ubiquitous Sensing with Minimal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14171)
*Xuanhao Luo,Zhizhen Li,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CSI-JEPA，一种用于Wi-Fi感知的自监督预测表示学习框架。该框架通过从未标记的CSI样本中学习可复用的时空谱表示，在7个真实任务上相比有监督基线准确率提升最高10.64个百分点，标签数据节省高达98.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CSI（信道状态信息）感知模型通常依赖于特定任务的监督训练，并且针对每个任务、设备、用户或环境都需要大量的标注数据。在实际部署中，未标记的CSI数据非常丰富，但标注数据的获取成本很高，这限制了现有模型的可扩展性。

Method: 提出了CSI-JEPA框架，这是一种自监督预测表示学习方法。核心方法包括：1) 沿时间和子载波维度对信道响应幅度窗口进行标记化（tokenize）；2) 通过从可见上下文预测被掩码信道区域的潜在特征来学习表示；3) 引入信道变化感知掩码策略，从具有更强局部时间和子载波域变化的区域采样预测目标；4) 预训练后冻结编码器作为骨干网络，并添加轻量级任务特定适配器用于下游任务。

Result: 在跨越不同目标和部署设置的7个真实世界Wi-Fi感知任务上进行了评估。结果表明，CSI-JEPA优于竞争基线，相比最先进的有监督Transformer实现了最高10.64个百分点的平均准确率提升，并实现了高达98.0%的同等预算标签节省。

Conclusion: CSI-JEPA成功实现了一种标签高效、多任务的Wi-Fi感知解决方案，证明了自监督学习在CSI感知领域的有效性。该方法能够充分利用大量未标记CSI数据，显著减少对昂贵标注数据的依赖，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Channel state information (CSI) provides a widely available sensing modality for human and environment perception, but existing CSI sensing models usually rely on task-specific supervised training and require substantial labeled data for each task, device, user, or environment. This limits their scalability in practical deployments where unlabeled CSI is abundant but labeled data is costly to collect. In this paper, we present CSI-JEPA, a self-supervised predictive representation learning framework for label-efficient, multi-task Wi-Fi sensing. CSI-JEPA learns reusable temporal-spectral representations from unlabeled CSI samples by predicting latent features of masked channel regions from visible context. To better match the physical structure of CSI, CSI-JEPA tokenizes channel-response amplitude windows along the time and subcarrier dimensions. It then introduces a channel variation-aware masking strategy that samples predictive targets from regions with stronger local temporal and subcarrier-domain variations. After pretraining, the encoder is frozen and used as a backbone, with lightweight task-specific adapters added for downstream sensing tasks. We evaluate CSI-JEPA on seven real-world Wi-Fi sensing tasks spanning diverse objectives and deployment settings. The results show that CSI-JEPA improves downstream sensing performance over competitive baselines, achieving up to 10.64 percentage points mean accuracy gain over state-of-the-art supervised Transformer and matched-budget label savings of up to 98.0%.

</details>


### [123] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督架构退火火框架（Joint-VAE），通过混合连续-离散潜在空间自动分离dMRI结构连接组中的采集变异与生物变异，在多中心数据集上实现了更优的站点聚类效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI结构连接组分析面临不同站点、扫描仪和采集协议导致的变异性问题。传统降维方法将所有方差建模为连续变量，导致采集效应被吸收到连续潜在空间中。现有的混合潜在空间模型虽结合离散和连续组件，但通常需要手动调整容量以确保离散组件捕获预期的变异性，这限制了其实用性。

Method: 引入无监督架构退火框架，在解码前对编码器输出进行架构退火，使模型能够在训练过程中自适应地平衡离散和连续潜在变量，从而消除手动调参需求。该方法扩展了Joint-VAE模型，采用混合连续-离散潜在空间联合建模平滑和类别结构。研究策划了包含N=7,416个结构连接组的数据集（年龄2-102岁，涵盖13项研究和25种独特采集参数组合），其中5,900例认知未受损、877例轻度认知障碍、639例阿尔茨海默病。

Result: 架构退火方法实现了更强的站点学习效果（ARI=0.53，p<0.05），优于标准VAE、PCA结合k-means聚类以及仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。Joint-VAE成功恢复了与扫描仪和协议差异对齐的聚类，证明了混合连续-离散潜在空间在捕获dMRI采集变异性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 采用架构退火而非基于损失的退火的混合连续-离散潜在空间，为捕获dMRI采集变异性提供了有效的无监督机制。通过联合建模平滑和类别结构，该方法能够分离采集相关效应与生物变异，为多中心神经影像研究提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Mode-Seeking RL: Trajectory-Balance Post-Training for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13935)
*Saba Ahmadi,Prasanna Parthasarathi,Yufei Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraFL方法，通过轨迹平衡目标解决了扩散语言模型在强化学习中存在的轨迹锁定问题，在推理和代码生成任务上实现了全面性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散语言模型后训练方法主要采用奖励最大化目标，但存在一个关键缺陷：轨迹锁定（trajectory locking）。具体表现为：基于奖励驱动的采样更新会将概率质量过度集中到狭窄的去噪路径集合上，导致在重复采样时对其他正确解的覆盖率降低。

Method: 提出了TraFL（Trajectory Flow baLancing）方法：1）采用轨迹平衡目标，将策略训练向以冻结参考模型为锚点的奖励倾斜目标分布；2）设计了与扩散模型兼容的序列级代理；3）引入了可学习的提示相关归一化技术。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，TraFL是唯一在所有基准-长度设置上都能超越基础模型的后训练方法，且性能优势随着采样预算增加而持续保持。在Minerva Math上保持优于基础模型，在LiveCodeBench的每个难度分层上都是最强方法。

Conclusion: TraFL成功解决了扩散语言模型中的轨迹锁定问题，通过轨迹平衡实现了更全面的正确解覆盖，证明了该方法在多种任务和设置下的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Diffusion language models are a promising alternative to autoregressive models, yet post-training methods for them largely adapt reward-maximizing objectives. We identify a central failure mode in this setting we call trajectory locking: sampled reward-driven updates over-concentrate probability mass onto a narrow set of denoising paths, reducing coverage of alternative correct solutions under repeated sampling. To address this, we propose TraFL (Trajectory Flow baLancing), a trajectory-balance objective that trains the policy toward a reward-tilted target distribution anchored to a frozen reference model. We make this practical for diffusion language models with a diffusion-compatible sequence-level surrogate and a learned prompt-dependent normalization. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, TraFL is the only evaluated post-training method that improves over the base model in every benchmark-length setting, with gains that persist as the sampling budget increases. The improvements transfer to held-out evaluations: TraFL stays above the base model on Minerva Math and is the strongest method on every LiveCodeBench difficulty split.

</details>


### [125] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台的框架，通过联邦协作在不共享私有数据的情况下，实现了跨机构分布式私有数据的LLM联合微调，在医疗和金融领域取得了接近集中式训练的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的发展主要依赖大规模公共数据集，但许多高价值信息（如医疗和金融领域的私有数据）由于隐私、法规和组织壁垒而无法共享。解锁这些分布式、非独立同分布（non-IID）的私有数据对于提升LLM的领域专业性和实际应用价值具有重要意义。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建框架，使各机构节点能够在不交换私有数据的前提下联合微调共享LLM。采用三种参数高效微调（PEFT）策略（LoRA、QLoRA和IA3），在反映机构数据异构性的非独立同分布设置下进行实验，使用医疗和金融领域的四个数据集（MedQA、MedMCQA、FPB和FiQA-SA）进行评估。

Result: 联邦微调的性能接近集中式训练，并且优于孤立的单机构学习。从绿色AI角度来看，QLoRA和IA3在有限精度损失的情况下提高了效率，验证了联邦PEFT作为数据无法共享情况下LLM适应方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调是一种可行的LLM适应方法，能够在保护隐私的前提下有效利用分布式私有数据，为解决数据孤岛问题提供了实用路径。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [126] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvolveMem，一种自演化记忆架构，通过LLM驱动的诊断模块自动优化检索配置，而非仅更新存储内容，在LoCoMo和MemBench基准上分别超越最强基线25.7%和18.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体的长期记忆系统存在局限性：存储内容动态演化，但检索基础设施（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）在部署后固定不变，缺乏自适应能力。作者认为真正的自适应记忆需要在两个层面协同演化：存储的知识和查询它的检索机制。

Method: 提出EvolveMem自演化记忆架构，将完整检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，由LLM驱动的诊断模块进行优化。每个演化轮次中，诊断模块读取每个问题的失败日志、识别根本原因并提出针对性配置调整；守卫式元分析器应用这些调整，并配备自动回退和停滞探索保障机制。这种闭环自演化实现了AutoResearch过程：系统自主对其架构进行迭代研究循环，替代人工配置调优。

Result: 在LoCoMo基准上，EvolveMem相比最强基线相对提升25.7%，相比最小基线相对提升78.0%。在MemBench基准上，超越最强基线18.9%。演化配置在跨基准迁移时呈现正向迁移而非灾难性迁移，表明自演化过程捕获了通用检索原则而非特定基准的启发式规则。系统能发现原始动作空间中不存在的新配置维度。

Conclusion: EvolveMem成功实现了记忆系统的双层协同演化，证明了自主架构优化的可行性。该方法从最小基线自主收敛，发现有效检索策略，并展现出良好的跨基准泛化能力，为LLM智能体的长期记忆管理提供了新的自适应范式。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Advantage in Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14235)
*Simranjeet Singh Dahia,Claudia Szabo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过在CHSH博弈和协作导航任务中的实证研究，首次为量子多智能体强化学习（QMARL）提供了可验证的量子优势证据：纠缠态使智能体接近Tsirelson极限（0.854），而无纠缠量子电路仅达到经典基准；在复杂任务中，量子演员与经典评论家结合的混合架构表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的量子多智能体强化学习（QMARL）研究大多缺乏可证明的经典基准，导致无法严格区分所观察到的性能提升是源于真实的量子优势还是算法巧合。本文旨在通过具有数学证明性能上界的基准任务，严谨地评估量子纠缠在智能体协调中的作用。

Method: 提出并评估了一个去中心化QMARL框架，使用变分量子电路（VQC）作为演员并共享纠缠态。实验主要在两方面进行：1）CHSH博弈（经典胜率上限数学证明为0.75）；2）协作导航任务（CoopNav）。对比了纠缠量子智能体、无纠缠量子智能体、经典MAA2C以及量子演员-经典评论家的混合配置。

Result: 在CHSH博弈中，纠缠QMARL智能体胜率接近Tsirelson极限（约0.854），显著超越经典上限0.75；无纠缠量子电路仅匹配经典基准。不同纠缠结构（Bell态）对性能影响各异，部分甚至损害性能。在CoopNav任务中，无纠缠QMARL成功率（~0.85）已是经典MAA2C（~0.40）的两倍；混合配置（量子演员+经典评论家）表现最佳，优于全经典和全量子方案。

Conclusion: 研究证实了量子纠缠是QMARL中实现协调优势的关键机制，而非量子电路本身。不同纠缠结构对协调效果有显著差异。混合架构（量子演员配对经典评论家）在实际协作任务中展现出最佳性能，为未来QMARL算法设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: We present an empirical evaluation of quantum entanglement in agent coordination within quantum multi agent reinforcement learning (QMARL). While QMARL has attracted growing interest recently, most prior work evaluates quantum policies without provable baselines, making it impossible to rigorously distinguish quantum advantage from algorithmic coincidence. We address this directly by evaluating a decentralized QMARL framework with variational quantum circuit (VQC) actors with shared entangled states. In the CHSH game, which has a mathematically proven classical performance ceiling of 0.75 win rate, we show that entangled QMARL agents approach the Tsirelson limit of 0.854, providing clear evidence of their quantum advantage. We show that unentangled quantum circuits match the classical baseline, confirming that entanglement and not the quantum circuit itself is the active coordination mechanism. We also explore the effect of specific entanglement structures, as some Bell states enable coordination gains while others actively harm performance. On cooperative navigation (CoopNav), QMARL without entanglement achieves $\sim2\times$ improvement in success rate over classical MAA2C ($\sim$0.85 versus $\sim$0.40), with the hybrid configuration, quantum actor paired with a classical centralised critic, outperforming both fully classical and fully quantum solutions. We present our experimental analysis and discuss future work.

</details>


### [128] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DAGS（数据增强博弈起点）的多智能体起始状态采样策略，通过从离线专家数据中采样中间状态来初始化强化学习数据收集，显著加速了大规模不完美信息博弈中的在线探索和均衡求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在《星际争霸》、《Dota》等大规模不完美信息博弈中，由于稀疏奖励和长视距带来的探索挑战，寻找近似纳什均衡在计算上仍然不可行。作者假设来自熟练人类的离线演示可以很好地覆盖与均衡博弈相关的高层策略，从而提出利用这些数据来加速探索。

Method: 提出Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS) 方法，这是一种多智能体起始状态采样策略。该方法从离线数据中采样中间状态，将强化学习的数据收集初始化在这些状态上，从而促进对策略相关子博弈的探索。此外，针对起始状态分布增强可能导致有偏均衡的问题，作者引入了多任务观察标志作为缓解措施。

Result: 在合成数据集以及可解析的长视距控制版Kuhn扑克、Goofspiel和一个反例博弈上的实验表明，在固定计算预算下，DAGS使正则化策略梯度方法在具有更具挑战性探索的博弈中实现了更低的可利用性（exploitability）。作者还证明了增强起始状态分布可能导致有偏均衡。

Conclusion: DAGS方法有效地解决了不完美信息博弈中的探索难题，能够加速均衡求解。同时，作者发布了新的基准环境，这些环境在保持可利用性可解析计算的同时，显著增加了现有OpenSpiel博弈的探索挑战和状态数量，为该领域提供了新的测试平台。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [129] [A Unified Geometric Framework for Weighted Contrastive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13943)
*Raphael Vock,Edouard Duchesnay,Benoit Dufumier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究建立了加权InfoNCE目标与距离几何问题的理论联系，揭示了权重方案决定了对比学习嵌入的几何可实现性、退化性或不一致性，为设计对比学习目标提供了原则性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习旨在通过学习反映相似性图的表示来保持样本间的关系结构，但由此产生的嵌入几何特性仍未被充分理解。现有的研究缺乏对不同加权方案如何影响嵌入几何特性的理论解释。

Method: 将加权InfoNCE目标解释为距离几何问题，其中加权方案指定了表示需要实现的目标几何结构。基于此视角，对监督和弱监督目标的最优嵌入进行了精确刻画，包括监督分类（SupCon和Soft SupCon）以及连续标签设置（y-Aware CL和X-CLR）。

Result: 在监督分类中，SupCon和Soft SupCon都会将每个类内的样本坍缩为单个原型。平衡的SupCon恢复经典的正单纯形几何，而类别不平衡时SupCon会产生依赖于类大小的非均匀类间相似性，Soft SupCon则无论类别是否不平衡都保持正单纯形几何。在连续标签设置中，y-Aware CL通常无法达到其熵最优解（除非标签位于超球面上），暴露了欧几里得标签权重与球面潜在相似性之间的不匹配；而几何一致性选择如欧几里得-欧几里得加权或X-CLR则允许唯一的最优嵌入。

Conclusion: 加权方案的选择决定了对比学习是否几何可实现、退化或不一致，不同加权方案会产生截然不同的几何特性。这为设计和选择对比学习目标提供了理论基础，强调了目标函数设计需要考虑几何一致性。

Abstract: Contrastive learning (CL) aims to preserve relational structure between samples by learning representations that reflect a similarity graph. Yet, the geometry of the resulting embeddings remains poorly understood. Here we show that weighted InfoNCE objectives can be interpreted as Distance Geometry Problems, where the weighting scheme specifies the target geometry to be realized by the representation. This viewpoint yields exact characterizations of the optimal embeddings for several supervised and weakly supervised objectives. In supervised classification, both SupCon and Soft SupCon (a dense relaxation of it where pairs from distinct classes have small non-zero similarity) collapse samples within each class to a single prototype. However, while balanced SupCon recovers the classical regular simplex geometry, class imbalance breaks this symmetry: SupCon induces non-uniform inter-class similarities depending on class sizes, whereas Soft SupCon preserves a regular simplex geometry regardless of class imbalance. In continuous-label settings, our framework reveals a different failure mode: y-Aware CL generally cannot attain its entropic optimum unless the labels lie on a hypersphere, exposing a mismatch between Euclidean label weights and spherical latent similarity. By contrast, geometrically consistent choices such as Euclidean-Euclidean weighting or X-CLR admit unique optimal embeddings. Our results show that the choice of weighting scheme determines whether contrastive learning is geometrically realizable, degenerate, or inconsistent, providing a principled framework for designing contrastive objectives.

</details>


### [130] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Collider-Bench基准测试，用于评估LLM代理在大型强子对撞机(LHC)实验分析复现任务上的能力，结果显示目前没有代理能可靠地超越有人类物理学家参与的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型代理评估基准很少能捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细节。大型强子对撞机的实验分析通常难以复现，因为公开的工具链只能近似实验合作组织内部使用的软件，而发表的论文不可避免地遗漏了忠实重建所需的实现细节。

Method: 1. 构建Collider-Bench基准，包含从LHC搜索中提取的任务集，要求代理将已发表的分析转换为可执行的模拟与选择流水线；2. 提交指定信号区域的碰撞事件产额预测，使用标准直方图指标评估连续保真度分数；3. 记录每个任务的计算成本；4. 使用LLM评估器分析代码库和完整会话轨迹，检测伪造、幻觉和重复等定性失效模式；5. 提供容器化沙盒和事件模拟工具。

Result: 在通用编程代理的能力阶梯上进行评估，结果显示平均而言没有任何代理能够可靠地超越有物理学家参与的解决方案。

Conclusion: Collider-Bench为评估LLM代理在复杂科学任务上的能力提供了一个具有挑战性的真实世界基准，揭示了当前代理在物理推理、领域知识应用和填补实现细节空白方面的局限性，为未来改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [131] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior，一种利用动作历史构建的时序先验，替代生成式机器人控制策略中的标准高斯源分布，显著提升了操作任务成功率和强化学习样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略在机器人控制中占主导地位，但标准的高斯源分布作为一个重要设计维度被忽视。受Re流中最优传输耦合能产生更直概率路径的启发，作者旨在探索源分布对策略性能的影响。

Method: 提出WarmPrior，一种简单且时序落地的先验分布，由易得的近期动作历史构建。将此先验替代标准高斯分布作为源分布，应用于行为克隆和先验空间强化学习。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上，WarmPrior持续提高了成功率。分析表明，收益源于概率路径显著变直，这与Re流中最优传输耦合的效果相呼应。在先验空间强化学习中，它改善了探索分布，提升了样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要且未被充分探索的设计轴，通过采用时序落地的WarmPrior可以显著提升策略性能。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [132] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个端到端的能耗核算框架，揭示了知识蒸馏中被忽视的教师端能耗成本，并给出了能效优化的实践设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常宣称知识蒸馏能获得更廉价、高效的模型，但忽略了数据生成、logit缓存和评估等教师端工作负载的完整能耗与资源成本，亟需全面的能耗评估方法。

Method: 提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，通过细粒度阶段式跟踪GPU设备功耗来测量蒸馏流水线的完整计算成本。实验中对两种常见蒸馏方法（基于logit的知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调）进行分阶段能耗测量，构建了能效-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 实证测量揭示了此前被忽视的蒸馏能耗成本，系统量化了两种蒸馏方法的完整能耗与排放，构建了显式包含完整流水线能耗影响的能效-质量权衡边界。

Conclusion: 基于测量分析，提出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法与超参数的实用设计规则，并开源了测量工具和核算协议，为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了标准化基础。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [133] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3将表格数据基础模型扩展至百万行训练数据，在TabArena基准上显著超越所有其他模型，引入测试时计算扩展机制，在关系数据和时间序列等任务上达到SOTA性能，同时速度比前代提升最高20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是科学和工业领域高价值预测问题的核心，现有基础模型在规模扩展方面存在局限。为了将表格基础模型的先进性能扩展到更大规模数据集（百万行级别），并大幅减少训练和推理时间，同时扩展到时间序列、关系数据和表格文本等多模态应用场景。

Method: 1) 仅使用合成数据进行预训练；2) 引入测试时计算扩展（test-time compute scaling）机制；3) 采用优化的KV缓存和行分块技术实现百万行规模扩展；4) 开发专用检查点TabPFN-TS-3用于时间序列；5) 提供API版本TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking)利用测试时计算。

Result: 1) TabArena基准上单次前向传播超越所有模型（包括调优和集成基线），在速度/性能前沿实现帕累托主导；2) TabPFN-3-Plus比非TabPFN模型高200+ Elo，最大数据集上高420 Elo；3) 比AutoGluon 1.5 extreme快10倍且性能更优；4) RelBenchV1关系数据新SOTA，TabSTAR表格文本SOTA；5) fev-bench时间序列排名第2；6) SHAP计算快120倍；7) 整体比TabPFN-2.5快20倍，单H100支持百万行。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3成功将表格基础模型扩展到工业级规模，在保持纯合成数据预训练、不依赖LLM/真实数据/外部模型的前提下，通过测试时计算扩展和架构优化，在多个模态实现SOTA性能，同时显著提升效率，推动表格预测领域前沿发展。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [134] [Neural Fields for NV-Center Inverse Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13988)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Tao Zhong,Yixun Hu,Nathalie P. de Leon,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对氮空位（NV）中心量子传感中的噪声谱反演问题，论文指出现有正向模型存在缺陷，并提出NeTMY方法（坐标神经场结合可微分物理模型），有效解决了自由密度优化中的中心坍塌失效模式，在稀疏源定位任务上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学传感中的逆问题通常采用手工设计的正则化器或基于仿真标签的监督网络，但当正向模型具有非线性、频谱耦合及物理敏感性时，这两种方法往往会失效。研究旨在解决NV中心量子传感中的磁场噪声谱反演难题。

Method: 论文首先将常用的标量/相干正向近似替换为张量幂和偶极算子，改善了反演景观。在此基础上提出NeTMY方法，将无摊销的坐标神经场与可微分NV正向模型耦合，并引入退火位置编码、多尺度优化、稀疏性门控机制以及谱保真度损失函数。

Result: 在使用修正算子生成的稀疏合成数据重建测试中，NeTMY在定位精度和分布度量指标上均达到基准测试中的最佳水平。机制实验表明，NeTMY并未直接执行原始密度空间梯度，其参数化机制能够平滑并重新分配更新，从而缓解了中心坍塌病理。

Conclusion: 研究证明了物理精确的正向模型与合适的神经场参数化相结合的重要性，将NV量子传感确立为探索物理忠实神经逆问题的有效测试平台。

Abstract: Inverse problems in scientific sensing are often solved with either hand-designed regularizers or supervised networks trained on simulated labels, yet both can fail when the forward model is nonlinear, spectrally coupled, and physically delicate. We study this issue for noise sensing based on nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers in diamond, where a quantum sensor measures magnetic-noise spectra generated by sparse spin sources. We show that replacing a common scalar/coherent forward approximation with a tensor power-summed dipolar operator changes the inverse landscape and exposes a center-collapse failure mode in free-density optimization. We propose NeTMY, an amortization-free coordinate neural field coupled to the differentiable NV forward model, with annealed positional encoding, multiscale optimization, sparsity/gating, and spectrum-fidelity losses. Across sparse synthetic reconstructions generated by the corrected operator, NeTMY achieves the best localization and distributional metrics in the tested benchmark. Mechanism experiments show that NeTMY does not directly execute the raw density-space gradient; its parameterization smooths and redistributes updates, mitigating the center-collapse pathology. These results position NV quantum sensing as a useful testbed for physics-faithful neural inverse problems.

</details>


### [135] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏混合专家模型中专家合并存在不可约循环的数学障碍（调和核），提出基于Hodge分解的HodgeCover方法，在激进压缩下实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无学习压缩方法在合并专家时面临一个关键障碍：三个专家可能两两兼容，但合并在一起时形成不可约循环，导致基于成对信号的评分方法无法识别哪些三元组可以联合合并。这一结构性盲点限制了现有压缩器的性能。

Method: 1. 将专家合并障碍建模为2-复形上的单纯形拉普拉斯算子，顶点为专家，边承载KL合并障碍，面承载三元组障碍；
2. 通过Hodge分解将边障碍信号分解，精确隔离出调和核；
3. 提出HodgeCover算法，贪婪覆盖调和关键边和三元组关键三角形；
4. 提出混合变体，将HodgeCover与现有权重剪枝方法结合。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE骨干网络上的实验表明：
1. HodgeCover在专家减少轴上匹配最先进无学习基线；
2. 在混合轴的激进压缩前沿领先；
3. 唯一地在所有四个Hodge分量上保持平衡的保留质量。

Conclusion: 暴露学习到的MoE结构的调和核改变了在最重要压缩区域中哪个压缩器获胜。这一发现为稀疏MoE模型的高效压缩提供了新的理论洞察和实践方法。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [136] [Support Before Frequency in Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13999)
*Adrian Müller,Antoine Gonon,Zebang Shen,Ya-Ping Hsieh,Niao He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上揭示了离散扩散模型在去噪过程中存在“先学数据支撑、后学数据频率”的学习层级，不同扩散机制（均匀扩散与吸收扩散）在有效性恢复上表现出显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在语言建模中日益具有竞争力，但其去噪目标如何组织学习过程尚不明确，特别是模型如何从针对全数据分布的目标中习得数据的结构信息。

Method: 针对均匀扩散和吸收（掩码）扩散，作者在最终去噪步骤的小噪声区域内进行了理论分析，证明了每个单token反向编辑可分解为主导尺度（决定是否向数据支撑移动）和更精细的系数（决定同一尺度内的相对概率）；并通过掩码语言扩散模型和合成正则语言任务进行了实验验证。

Result: 理论分析表明，恢复有效性结构仅需学习反向概率的正确数量级，而恢复数据频率则需要系数级别的精确估计；均匀扩散呈现出改善有效性、保持有效性和恶化有效性的三分现象，而吸收扩散的主导阶质量集中在改善有效性的移动上；实验证实数据支撑定位早于支撑内频率排序，且均匀扩散与吸收扩散的对比符合预测的速率分离。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型的学习过程存在内在层级：模型首先学习数据的支撑（如语法有效性），随后才学习支撑内的频率分布；不同的扩散机制导致了不同的有效性恢复模式。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are increasingly competitive for language modeling, yet it remains unclear how their denoising objectives organize learning. Although these objectives target the full data distribution, we show that the exact reverse process induces a hierarchy between coarse support information and finer frequency information. For uniform and absorbing (a.k.a. masking) diffusion, we prove that, in the small-noise regime of the final denoising steps, each single-token reverse edit decomposes into a leading scale, determined by whether it moves toward the data support (e.g., grammatically valid sentences), and a finer coefficient, determining relative probabilities within the same scale. Thus, recovering validity structure only requires learning the correct order of magnitude of reverse probabilities, whereas recovering data frequencies requires coefficient-level estimation. The separation is mechanism-dependent: uniform diffusion exhibits a trichotomy into validity-improving, validity-preserving, and validity-worsening edits, while absorbing diffusion places its leading-order mass on validity-improving moves. Experiments on a masked language diffusion model and synthetic regular-language tasks support these predictions: support-localization emerges earlier than within-support frequency ranking, and the contrast between uniform and absorbing diffusion matches the predicted rate separation. Together, our results suggest that discrete diffusion models learn data support before data frequencies.

</details>


### [137] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dywave，一种针对IoT感知信号的动态标记化框架，通过小波分解和语义边界识别实现自适应压缩，在准确率提升12%的同时将输入标记长度减少75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统持续收集的感知信号具有非平稳和多尺度特性，标准标记化技术难以有效处理，需要一种能够与内在时间结构和物理事件对齐的紧凑输入表示方法。

Method: Dywave利用基于小波的分层分解技术，识别对应底层语义事件的有意义时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间同时保持时间连贯性，构建与内在时间结构对齐的紧凑输入表示。

Result: 在五个真实IoT感知数据集上的实验表明，Dywave在活动识别、压力评估和附近物体检测任务中准确率比现有最优方法提升高达12%，同时在主流序列模型中将输入标记长度减少高达75%，计算效率显著提高。此外，Dywave对域偏移和变化序列长度表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave有效解决了IoT感知信号的标记化挑战，通过动态标记化实现了准确性和效率的双重提升，为IoT智能应用提供了更优的信号处理方案。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [138] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出R2R2正则化方法，通过非中心化目标解决自预测学习在高数据重用场景下的表示层过拟合问题，在TD7上提升约22%并基于SimbaV2创造了新的SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的现实机器人强化学习场景中，高数据重用（高UTD比率）虽提升效率但会导致过拟合。现有工作主要关注critic偏差，而自预测学习（SPL）在高UTD机制下的表示层不稳定性仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出R2R2（Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction）正则化方法。理论分析发现标准零中心化与SPL的谱特性冲突，据此设计了非中心化目标函数。在SPL原生算法TD7上验证，并将SPL模块集成到SimbaV2中构建SimbaV2-SPL以展示方法的正交性。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上的实验表明：R2R2有效缓解过拟合；在UTD=20时，TD7性能提升约22%；在SimbaV2-SPL基础上获得额外增益，SimbaV2-SPL本身达到新的SOTA水平。

Conclusion: R2R2成功解决了高UTD场景下SPL的表示不稳定问题，与现有改进方法正交，可广泛应用于SPL类算法，为数据稀缺的机器人强化学习提供了有效的正则化方案。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [139] [Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention: Learning When to Write by Predicting Future Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14037)
*Gergely Szilvasy,Manuel Faysse,Maria Lomeli,Matthijs Douze,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Loïc Cabannes,Wen-tau Yih,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SP-KV（自剪枝键值注意力）机制，通过轻量级效用预测器动态剪枝KV缓存，实现3-10倍的压缩比，显著提升内存效率和推理速度，且几乎不影响模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代测试时计算和智能体范式下，语言模型处理的序列越来越长，Transformer架构的高效文本生成日益受到键值（KV）缓存的内存占用和带宽限制。

Method: 引入一种轻量级效用预测器对每个键值对进行评分；近期的KV通过本地窗口保持可用，而较旧的键值对只有当预测效用超过给定阈值时才会被写入缓存并参与全局注意力。LLM与效用预测器仅通过下一词元预测损失进行端到端联合训练，并从预训练LLM检查点适配而来。

Result: 该方法不强制固定压缩比，而是根据输入动态稀疏化，通常将KV缓存大小减少3到10倍（长序列往往更具可压缩性）；在大幅改善内存使用和解码速度的同时，验证损失及广泛的下游任务性能几乎没有下降。此外，该方法还揭示了结构化的层和头特定的稀疏模式。

Conclusion: SP-KV不仅是一种有效的KV缓存缩减机制，其揭示的层和头特定稀疏模式还可用于指导混合局部-全局注意力架构的设计。

Abstract: Under modern test-time compute and agentic paradigms, language models process ever-longer sequences. Efficient text generation with transformer architectures is increasingly constrained by the Key-Value cache memory footprint and bandwidth. To address this limitation, we introduce Self-Pruned Key-Value Attention (SP-KV), a mechanism designed to predict future KV utility in order to reduce the size of the long-term KV cache. This strategy operates at a fine granularity: a lightweight utility predictor scores each key-value pair, and while recent KVs are always available via a local window, older pairs are written in the cache and used in global attention only if their predicted utility surpasses a given threshold.
