On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [213] [An Amortized Efficiency Threshold for Comparing Neural and Heuristic Solvers in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14624)
*Sohaib Afifi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出摊销效率阈值（AET）框架，反驳了“神经组合优化求解器训练耗能故整体低效”的批评：当部署规模足够大时，训练成本可被摊销，神经求解器在总能耗或碳排放上可优于传统元启发式算法。实验显示在MTVRP环境（n=20）下，AET约为1.58×10^5个实例，单实例能耗比为0.41。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对学术界对神经组合优化求解器的常见批评——因GPU训练能耗高而认为其整体能效低于CPU元启发式算法，本文指出该批评的逻辑缺陷：训练成本是固定的一次性投入，而传统方法每次求解都有能耗，两者需在确定的部署规模下才可比较。现有研究缺乏量化框架来评估神经求解器何时在能耗/碳排放上“回本”。

Method: 提出摊销效率阈值（AET）理论框架：定义在给定解质量约束下，神经求解器总能耗/碳排放与启发式基线持平时的最小部署实例数；证明当神经求解器单实例更优时，累计能耗比收敛到严格小于1的常数，且与训练成本度量方式无关；引入硬件制造碳足迹的对称摊销项。实验上，在MTVRP环境（n=20，19个问题变体，5个训练种子）中以PyVRP的HGS为基线进行实证测量。

Result: 理论结果：当神经求解器单实例能耗更优时，累计能耗比极限为小于1的常数，该极限独立于训练成本度量方式。实证结果：MTVRP（n=20）场景下，AET约为1.58×10^5个部署实例，即超过此规模后神经求解器总能耗更低；单实例能耗比为0.41，说明在测试的实例规模下神经求解器每次求解能耗约为基线的41%。

Conclusion: 神经组合优化求解器的能效评价必须考虑部署规模；AET框架为该评价提供了理论与测量基础。在中等规模问题上，神经求解器在足够大的部署规模下可实现净能效优势。贡献在于通用框架、开源工具与测量协议，更大规模问题的结构收敛性留待未来研究。

Abstract: A common critique of neural combinatorial-optimization solvers is that they are less energy-efficient than CPU metaheuristics, given the operational energy cost of training them on GPUs. This paper examines the inferential step from "training is expensive" to "neural solvers are net-inefficient", which is where the critique actually goes wrong. Training the network costs a large fixed amount of GPU energy; running the metaheuristic costs a small amount of CPU energy on every instance, repeated as long as the solver is deployed. The two are not commensurable until a deployment volume is fixed. We define the Amortized Efficiency Threshold (AET) as the deployment volume above which a neural solver breaks even with a heuristic baseline in total energy or carbon, under an explicit constraint on solution quality. We show that the cumulative-energy ratio between the two solvers tends to a constant strictly below one whenever the network wins per-instance, and that this limit does not depend on how the training cost was measured. An embodied-carbon term amortizes hardware fabrication symmetrically on both sides. We instantiate the framework on the Multi-Task VRP (MTVRP) environment at n=20 customers across 19 problem variants and five training seeds, with HGS via PyVRP as the heuristic baseline. The measured crossover sits near $1.58 \times 10^5$ deployed instances; the per-instance ratio is 0.41, reflecting the moderate size of the instances tested. The contribution is the framework, the open instrumentation, and the measurement protocol; structural convergence of the ratio at larger problem sizes is left to future empirical work.

</details>


### [214] [Action-Inspired Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14631)
*Eshwar R. A.,Debnath Pal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGMs框架，通过引入轻量级势能函数V_φ对桥接样本进行在线评分和重要性加权，选择性惩罚无效传输路径，在几乎不增加参数和零推理开销的情况下提升了生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的桥接匹配方法在传输过程中对每个随机转移分配统一的回归权重，而忽略了样本可能位于结构连贯轨迹上或退化路径上的差异。这种不加区分的统一处理会导致模型对无效或信息量低的传输路径进行不必要的优化，从而限制生成性能。

Method: 提出双网络生成框架AGMs，引入仅占主网络约1.4%参数量的轻量级可学习标量势能函数V_φ。V_φ在线对桥接样本进行评分，通过stop-gradient屏障导出重要性权重来调节drift目标函数，防止两个网络间的对抗性反馈。训练结束后V_φ被完全丢弃，仅使用EMA drift进行标准Euler-Maruyama积分推理。该方法无需迭代半桥拟合或辅助SDE求解器，是即插即用的增强方案。

Result: 通过学习势能选择性惩罚信息量低的传输路径，在保真度和覆盖率等生成质量指标上取得一致的改进效果。V_φ不增加推理图开销，且对训练流程的侵入性极小。

Conclusion: AGMs框架成功解决了桥接匹配中统一权重分配的局限性，以极低的额外成本实现了性能提升。其即插即用的特性使其可便捷地集成到任何桥接匹配训练循环中，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce Action-Inspired Generative Models (AGMs), a dual-network generative framework motivated by the observation that existing bridge-matching methods assign uniform regression weight to every stochastic transition in the transport landscape, regardless of whether a given bridge sample lies along a structurally coherent trajectory or a degenerate one. We address this by introducing a lightweight learned scalar potential $V_φ$ that scores bridge samples online and modulates the drift objective via importance weights derived through a stop-gradient barrier -- preventing adversarial feedback between the two networks whilst preserving $V_φ$'s guiding signal. Crucially, $V_φ$ comprises only $\sim$1.4% of the primary drift network's parameter count, adds no overhead to the inference graph, and requires no iterative half-bridge fitting or auxiliary stochastic differential equation (SDE) solvers: it is a plug-and-play enhancement to any bridge-matching training loop. At inference, $V_φ$ is discarded entirely, leaving standard Euler-Maruyama integration of the exponential moving average (EMA) drift. We demonstrate that selectively penalising uninformative transport paths through the learned potential yields consistent improvements in generation quality across fidelity and coverage metrics.

</details>


### [215] [DRL-STAF: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Framework for State-Aware Forecasting of Complex Multivariate Hidden Markov Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14632)
*Manrui Jiang,Jingru Huang,Yong Chen,Chen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DRL-STAF框架，结合深度神经网络和强化学习，在预测多元隐马尔可夫过程的同时估计隐状态，解决了传统HMM难以处理非线性观测和状态空间爆炸的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多元隐马尔可夫过程预测方法存在明显局限：深度学习方法虽预测精度高但缺乏显式状态建模能力；隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）虽提供可解释的隐状态，但难以处理复杂非线性发射函数且面临状态空间爆炸的可扩展性挑战。

Method: 提出DRL-STAF（基于深度强化学习的状态感知预测框架）：1) 利用深度神经网络建模复杂非线性发射函数；2) 采用强化学习估计离散隐状态，避免依赖预定义的状态转移结构，灵活适应多样的时间动态；3) 有效缓解了典型多元HMM方法面临的状态空间爆炸问题。

Result: 大量实验表明，DRL-STAF在大多数情况下优于HMM变体、独立深度学习模型以及现有的DL-HMM混合方法，同时能够提供可靠的隐状态估计。

Conclusion: DRL-STAF成功实现了预测精度与状态可解释性的统一，为多元隐马尔可夫过程预测提供了一种兼顾性能与可解释性的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting multivariate hidden Markov processes is challenging due to nonlinear and nonstationary observations, latent state transitions, and cross-sequence dependencies. While deep learning methods achieve strong predictive accuracy, they typically lack explicit state modeling, whereas Hidden Markov Models (HMMs) provide interpretable latent states but struggle with complex nonlinear emissions and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose DRL-STAF, a Deep Reinforcement Learning based STate-Aware Forecasting framework that jointly predicts next-step observations and estimates the corresponding hidden states for complex multivariate hidden Markov processes. Specifically, DRL-STAF models complex nonlinear emissions using deep neural networks and estimates discrete hidden states using reinforcement learning, reducing the reliance on predefined transition structures and enabling flexible adaptation to diverse temporal dynamics. In particular, DRL-STAF mitigates the state-space explosion encountered by typical multivariate HMM-based methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DRL-STAF outperforms HMM variants, standalone deep learning models, and existing DL-HMM hybrids in most cases, while also providing reliable hidden-state estimates.

</details>


### [216] [Unbiased and Second-Order-Free Training for High-Dimensional PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14643)
*Jaemin Seo,Surin Lee,Jae Yong Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了BSDE方法中Euler-Maruyama离散化导致的内在偏差，并提出了一种无偏、无需二阶导数的训练框架，在消除偏差的同时保持了计算优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于倒向随机微分方程(BSDE)的深度学习方法是求解高维偏微分方程(PDE)的重要方法。常用的Euler-Maruyama(EM)时间离散化会在训练损失中引入内在偏差，而高阶方案(如Heun)虽然能消除偏差，但会重新引入二阶空间导数并带来大量计算开销。因此需要一种既能消除偏差又无需二阶导数的方法。

Method: 对EM离散化引起的损失偏差进行原理性分析，在此基础上提出一种无偏且无需二阶导数的训练框架，在保持BSDE方法计算优势的同时消除EM带来的偏差。

Result: 成功构建了一个无偏的训练框架，既避免了EM方法的内在偏差，又无需计算二阶导数，从而保留了BSDE方法的计算效率优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的无偏训练框架有效地解决了EM离散化带来的偏差问题，同时避免了高阶方案的计算开销，为基于BSDE的高维PDE求解提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning methods based on backward stochastic differential equations (BSDEs) have emerged as competitive alternatives to physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) for solving high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs). By leveraging probabilistic representations, BSDE approaches can avoid the curse of dimensionality and often admit second-order-free training objectives that do not require explicit Hessian evaluations. It has recently been established that the commonly used Euler-Maruyama (EM) time discretization induces an intrinsic bias in BSDE training losses. While high-order schemes such as Heun can fully eliminate this bias, such schemes re-introduce second-order spatial derivatives and incur substantial computational overhead. In this work, we provide a principled analysis of EM-induced loss bias and propose an unbiased, second-order-free training framework that preserves the computational advantages of BSDE methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/seojaemin22/Un-EM-BSDE.

</details>


### [217] [Slower Generalization, Faster Memorization: A Sweet Spot in Algorithmic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14659)
*Shin So,Kyelim Lee,Albert No*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在结构化输出任务中，数据量并非越多越好：超过临界阈值后，继续增加数据反而会减慢模型收敛速度，揭示了规则泛化与训练收敛之间的分离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于“grokking”现象的临界数据规模理论认为，一旦训练数据足以识别底层规则，更多的数据应加速验证集收敛。本文旨在挑战这一直觉，探究在受控的结构化输出任务中，数据规模与收敛速度之间的真实关系。

Method: 研究者使用Needleman--Wunsch (NW) 矩阵生成任务作为受控实验环境，训练小型Transformer模型。通过系统地调整数据集规模，观察模型达到高验证准确率所需的梯度更新步数，并以乘法任务作为基线进行对比分析。

Result: 在NW任务中，模型在中等规模数据集上达到高验证准确率的速度最快，而非最大规模数据集；超过此“最佳点”后，泛化仍可实现但需要更多梯度更新。相反，在部分验证能力首次出现的阶段，较大数据集反而能用更少更新达到高训练准确率。乘法基线任务未表现出相同的阈值后减速现象。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了触发泛化的临界数据规模与优化收敛速度的最佳数据规模是分离的。在特定结构化输出任务中，学习规则与完成精确拟合可能发生背离，打破了“数据越多收敛越快”的传统直觉。

Abstract: Critical-data-size accounts of grokking suggest a natural post-threshold intuition: once training data is sufficient to identify the underlying rule, additional data should accelerate validation convergence. We show that this intuition can fail in a controlled structured-output task. In Needleman--Wunsch (NW) matrix generation, small Transformers reach high validation exact-match accuracy fastest at an intermediate dataset size, not at the largest one. Past this dataset-size sweet spot, generalization remains achievable but requires more gradient updates. Conversely, in the regime where partial validation competence first appears, larger datasets can require fewer updates to reach high training accuracy, suggesting that emerging rule structure can accelerate fitting beyond example-wise memorization. A multiplication baseline does not show the same post-threshold slowdown. These results separate the critical data size for the onset of generalization from the dataset size that optimizes update-based convergence, and identify structured-output tasks where learning the rule and completing exact-fitting can diverge.

</details>


### [218] [AQKA: Active Quantum Kernel Acquisition Under a Shot Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14672)
*Jian Xu,Chao Li,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应量子核获取方法（AQKA），通过闭式理论指导的动态测量次数分配，显著降低量子核估计的测量成本，在真实量子硬件上相比均匀分配提升14-32个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计N×N量子核需要Θ(N²S)次测量，这是在近期量子硬件上部署的主要瓶颈。现有方法（Nyström-QKE、ShoFaR等）仅对条目进行子采样，但在选定子集内仍均匀分配测量次数，忽略了不同条目对下游分类器影响的差异。

Method: 提出AQKA方法，核心为闭式成对获取理论：最优测量次数s*_{ij} ∝ |g_{ij}|√(K_{ij}(1-K_{ij}))，其中g_{ij}为显式梯度（KRR）或通过包络定理导出（SVM）；建立了完整的预算区域分解框架，明确了各分配策略的适用条件；开发了修正的稀疏感知Cauchy-Schwarz收敛率ρ≤2m/N及更紧的SVM上界。

Result: 在预算受限区域（B≲16n_pairs），AQKA在稀疏敏感KRR上显著优于均匀分配，N从225增至1000时优势从+8扩大到+25个百分点；在ibm_pittsburgh（156量子比特）硬件核上达到+26-32个百分点优势；首次实现多种子在线自适应测量分配，在ibm_aachen上N=20时获得+17.0±4.8分（3.5σ，5种子），在ibm_berlin上N=30时获得+14.0±8.5分。Nyström-QKE在高饱和预算下的低秩重建场景占优，ShoFaR仅在极端低预算下有竞争力。

Conclusion: 填补了量子核学习中测量次数非均匀分配的理论空白，提供了成对获取的闭式解和完整的预算区域分解指南，在真实量子硬件上验证了自适应分配的显著优势，为量子核方法在近期设备上的实用化部署提供了重要理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Estimating an $N \times N$ quantum kernel from circuit fidelities requires $Θ(N^2 S)$ measurement shots, the dominant bottleneck for deployment on near-term hardware. Existing budget-saving methods (Nyström-QKE, ShoFaR, kernel-target alignment) sub-sample \emph{which} entries to measure but allocate shots \emph{uniformly} within their chosen subset, ignoring how much each entry drives the downstream classifier. We close this gap with two contributions. \textbf{First, a complete regime decomposition} for shot-budgeted quantum kernel learning: a principled menu of when each allocator wins. Our method, \emph{AQKA}, dominates the budget-limited regime ($B \lesssim 16 n_{\mathrm{pairs}}$) on sparse-sensitivity KRR, with the gap \emph{growing} from $+8$ to $+25$ pts over uniform as $N$ scales $225{\to}1000$ and reaching $+26$--$32$ pts on an \texttt{ibm\_pittsburgh} (156-qubit Heron) hardware kernel; Nyström-QKE wins at saturating budgets on planted-sparse via low-rank reconstruction; ShoFaR is competitive only at extreme low budgets. \textbf{Second, a closed-form pair-level acquisition theory}: $s_{ij}^{\star} \propto |g_{ij}|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$ with explicit gradient $g_{ij}$ for KRR (Lemma~1, $|β_iα_j+β_jα_i|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$) and SVM via the envelope theorem ($|η_i^*η_j^*|\sqrt{K_{ij}(1-K_{ij})}$); a \emph{corrected} sparsity-aware Cauchy--Schwarz rate $ρ\le 2m/N$ matching empirics (vs.\ the naive $m^2/N^2$); an explicit-constant plug-in regret bound (Theorem~2); and a tighter SVM ceiling $ρ^{\mathrm{SVM}} \le m_{\mathrm{sv}}^2/N^2$. We close with the first multi-seed live online adaptive shot allocation on quantum hardware: $+17.0 \pm 4.8$ pts at $N{=}20$ on \texttt{ibm\_aachen} ($3.5σ$, 5 seeds), with the advantage holding at $N{=}30$ at higher budget on \texttt{ibm\_berlin} ($+14.0 \pm 8.5$ pts, 5 seeds).

</details>


### [219] [Spontaneous symmetry breaking and Goldstone modes for deep information propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14685)
*Nabil Iqbal,T. Anderson Keller,Yue Song,Takeru Miyato,Max Welling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将物理学中的戈德斯通模式概念引入深度学习，证明具有连续对称性等变的神经网络能够支持类似戈德斯通模式的自由度，从而实现稳定的信号传播，无需残差连接或归一化等架构稳定器，有效提升了前馈网络的可训练性和RNN在长序列建模上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理系统中，连续对称性的自发破缺会产生戈德斯通模式，这种激发态能够支持长距离和长时间的信息相干传播。作者受到这一物理现象的启发，旨在探索深度神经网络中是否存在类似的机制。研究的动机在于探究当神经网络的内部层具有连续对称性等变性质时，是否也能支持类似的自由度，从而为解决深度网络中的信号传播和长时记忆问题提供新的物理视角和解决方案。

Method: 作者从理论物理与深度学习的交叉视角出发，研究内部层具有连续对称性等变性质的深度神经网络。首先，通过解析推导论证这类网络中存在类似于戈德斯通模式的自由度。随后，通过实证分析验证这些自由度能够支持信号在深度方向和循环迭代中进行相干传播。研究对比了有无残差连接或归一化等架构稳定器的情况，以此验证该机制在信号稳定传播中的独立作用。

Result: 研究结果表明，这种类戈德斯通模式的自由度为信号在网络中的稳定传播提供了机制，使其不依赖于残差连接或归一化层等传统架构技巧。具体而言，在前馈网络中，该机制提高了网络的可训练性并增加了各层表示的多样性；在循环神经网络（RNN）和门控循环单元（GRU）等循环设置中，该机制能够支持长期记忆，通过在循环迭代中传播信息，显著提升了模型在长序列建模任务上的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究成功地将物理学中的戈德斯通模式概念推广到深度学习领域，揭示了神经网络内部对称性与信息传播能力之间的深刻联系。结论指出，通过对称性等变设计引入的类戈德斯通自由度，为解决深度神经网络的信号衰减问题提供了一种根本性的、非架构式的解决方案，不仅加深了对神经网络动力学的理论理解，也为设计更高效、更稳定的网络架构提供了新的指导原则。

Abstract: In physical systems, whenever a continuous symmetry is spontaneously broken, the system possesses excitations called Goldstone modes, which allow coherent information propagation over long distances and times. In this work, we study deep neural networks whose internal layers are equivariant under a continuous symmetry and may therefore support analogous Goldstone-like degrees of freedom. We demonstrate, both analytically and empirically, that these degrees of freedom enable coherent signal propagation across depth and recurrent iterations, providing a mechanism for stable information flow without relying on architectural stabilizers such as residual connections or normalization. In feedforward networks, this results in improved trainability and representational diversity across layers. In recurrent settings, we demonstrate the same mechanism is valuable for long-term memory by propagating information over recurrent iterations, thereby improving performance of RNNs and GRUs on long-sequence modeling tasks.

</details>


### [220] [ReMIA: a Powerful and Efficient Alternative to Membership Inference Attacks against Synthetic Data Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14686)
*Davide Scassola,Andrea Coser,Sebastiano Saccani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReMIA（相对成员推理攻击），一种实用的隐私评估指标，仅需两次合成数据生成器（SDG）训练运行和不超过原始训练集规模的辅助数据，即可达到与最先进MIA方法相当的敏感度，同时大幅提升了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的成员推理攻击（MIA）虽然强大但不实用，因为它们依赖影子模型，需要数百次SDG训练运行，且需要比原始训练集大数倍的辅助数据；而快速的代理指标（如DCR）对MIA风险的敏感性有限。因此需要一种既实用又敏感的隐私评估方法。

Method: ReMIA不直接预测记录是否在训练集中，而是从两个源数据集生成两个合成数据集，然后测量分类器能否识别记录来自哪个源数据集。该方法仅需两次SDG训练运行，且所需额外数据不超过原始训练集规模。

Result: 在多个表格数据集和SDG上的实验表明，ReMIA的敏感度与最先进的MIA方法相当，同时在实用性方面显著优于现有方法。此外，研究发现SDG可以实现传统基于噪声的匿名化方法无法匹配的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: ReMIA为合成数据隐私评估提供了一种实用且敏感的解决方案，使研究人员能够在实际条件下有效评估隐私风险，同时揭示了SDG在隐私-效用权衡方面的独特优势。

Abstract: Tabular data sharing under privacy constraints is increasingly important for research and collaboration. Synthetic data generators (SDGs) are a promising solution, but synthetic data remains vulnerable to attacks, such as membership inference attacks (MIAs), which aim to determine whether a specific record was part of the training data. State-of-the-art MIAs are powerful but impractical: they rely on shadow modeling, requiring hundreds of SDG training runs, and need auxiliary data several times larger than the original training set. Fast proxy metrics like distance to closest record (DCR) are efficient but have limited sensitivity to MIA risk. We introduce ReMIA (Relative Membership Inference Attack), a practical privacy metric that requires only two SDG training runs and additional data no larger than the original training set. Rather than predicting whether a record was in the training set, ReMIA generates two synthetic datasets from two source datasets and measures whether a classifier can identify which source a record came from. Experiments across multiple tabular datasets and SDGs show that ReMIA has a sensitivity comparable to state-of-the-art MIAs while being substantially more practical. We further observe that SDGs can achieve privacy-utility trade-offs that traditional noise-based anonymization methods do not match. Code is available at https://github.com/aindo-com/remia.

</details>


### [221] [The Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity Tradeoff in SAEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14694)
*Tommaso Mencattini,Francesco Montagna,Francesco Locatello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏自编码器（SAE）中忠实性、效率与可解释性之间的内在权衡关系，证明多义性是由数据分布决定的根本性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）虽然在最小化失真和速率方面表现良好，但往往无法学习到单义性表示（即高度可解释的特征），这限制了其在机制可解释性研究中的应用价值。为了理解并解决这一问题，作者旨在探究这一矛盾的根源及其特性。

Method: 作者首先在玩具模型假设下，从理论和实证角度分析了限制SAE为单义性表示对速率和失真的影响；随后假设输入观测背后存在生成模型，分析了训练数据分布（特别是特征共现概率）对最优SAE多义性程度的影响；最后将分析扩展到真实场景，推导了多义性度量应满足的必要条件，并在大语言模型上训练的SAE上对现有代理指标进行了基准测试。

Result: 研究发现，限制SAE学习单义性表示必然会导致速率和失真的增加。最优SAE的多义性程度由训练数据分布决定，特别是特征共现的概率。多义性本质上是一个数据问题，需要从数据和优化层面共同解决。

Conclusion: 多义性是由数据生成过程决定的问题，不能仅通过架构和优化层面的调整完全解决。在设计SAE和评估其可解释性时，必须充分考虑数据分布特性的影响。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) that can accurately reconstruct their input (minimizing distortion) by making efficient use of few features (minimizing the rate) often fail to learn monosemantic representations (highly interpretable), limiting their usefulness for mechanistic interpretability. In this paper, we characterise this tension in learning faithful, efficient, and interpretable explanations, introducing the Rate-Distortion-Polysemanticity tradeoff in SAEs. Under toy-modeling assumptions, we theoretically and empirically show that restricting the SAE to be monosemantic necessarily comes with an increase in rate and distortion. Assuming a generative model behind the input observations, we further demonstrate that the degree of polysemanticity of optimal SAEs is determined by the training data distribution, especially by the probability of features to co-occur. Finally, we extend the analysis to real-world settings by deriving necessary conditions that a polysemanticity measure should satisfy when the data-generating process is unknown, and we benchmark existing proxy metrics on SAEs trained on Large Language Models. Taken together, our findings show that polysemanticity is a data problem that should be accounted for when addressing it at the architectural and optimization level.

</details>


### [222] [NeuroAtlas: Benchmarking Foundation Models for Clinical EEG and Brain-Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14698)
*Konstantinos Kontras,Trui Osselaer,Stylianos G. Mouslech,Angeliki-Ilektra Karaiskou,Guido Gagliardi,Thomas Strypsteen,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Anku Rani,Maarten Vanmarcke,Miguel Bhagubai,Chanakya Ekbote,Jaedong Hwang,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了迄今为止最大的EEG基准NeuroAtlas（42个数据集，26万小时数据），系统评估了EEG基础模型与通用时间序列基础模型。发现EEG专用模型并未显著优于通用模型，标准机器学习指标不足以评估临床价值，且当前模型尚未实现统一的EEG模型的承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EEG基础模型的评估存在显著问题：不同研究使用不同的数据集、EEG特定预处理方法和评估指标，导致结果难以比较且常常掩盖了临床相关性。现有评估缺乏对EEG领域临床实用性的充分考量，亟需一个全面、标准化的基准来客观评估EEG基础模型的真实性能。

Method: 构建了NeuroAtlas基准平台，包含42个数据集和26万小时EEG数据，覆盖临床EEG（癫痫、睡眠医学、脑龄估计）和脑机接口领域。每个任务包含多个数据集，并设计了针对性的临床评估指标。系统比较了EEG专用基础模型与监督学习基线以及通用时间序列基础模型的性能表现。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）EEG专用基础模型并未持续优于通用时间序列基础模型，后者既无EEG专用架构也无EEG预训练；（2）标准机器学习指标不足以评估临床实用性，需要采用事件级决策质量、睡眠图衍生特征、脑龄差距等更合适的临床评估指标；（3）模型排名和性能在同一领域内可能存在显著差异。预训练模型整体表现相当，仅有少数模型具有微弱优势。

Conclusion: 当前基础模型尚未实现开箱即用的统一EEG模型的承诺。NeuroAtlas揭示了现有模型与理想统一模型之间的差距，为下一代统一EEG基础模型的研发提供了所需的数据集和评估指标体系，为该领域的未来发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) promise to extract unified representations that generalize across downstream tasks. They have emerged across fields, including electroencephalography (EEG), but it is less clear how effective they are in this particular field. Published evaluations differ in datasets, in the EEG-specific preprocessing that might influence reported results, and in the reported metrics, frequently obscuring the clinical relevance in EEG. We introduce NeuroAtlas, the largest EEG benchmark to date: 42 datasets and 260k hours covering clinical EEG (epilepsy, sleep medicine, brain age estimation) and brain-computer interfaces, and include multiple datasets per task along with bespoke clinical evaluation metrics. Besides evaluating EEG-FMs with respect to supervised baselines, we present results from generic time-series FMs. We report three findings. First, EEG-specific FMs do not consistently outperform time-series FMs, which have neither EEG-focused architectures nor been pretrained on EEG. Second, standard machine learning metrics are insufficient to assess clinical utility: thus, we thoroughly evaluate more appropriate measures such as the quality of event-level decision-making, hypnogram-derived features, and the brain-age gap in the domains of epilepsy, sleep, and brain age, respectively. Third, model rankings and performance can vary substantially within domains. We conclude that pretrained models perform largely on par, with only narrow advantages for a few, and that current models do not yet deliver on the promise of an out-of-the-box unified EEG model. NeuroAtlas exposes this gap and provides the datasets and metrics for the next generation of unified EEG FMs.

</details>


### [223] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了任务感知层剪枝(TALE)通过移除造成分布失真的层，将OOD输入的表示几何特性重新对齐到任务适应的几何结构，从而仅提升OOD性能而非ID性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作（如TALE）表明任务感知层剪枝能提升特定任务的模型性能，但尚不清楚这种提升在何时发生以及为何发生。本文旨在深入探究该现象背后的机理与条件。

Method: 1. 在受控多项式回归任务和大语言模型上进行系统实验；2. 对比分析ID与OOD输入在层级范数和成对距离上的表征差异；3. 提出几何解释框架：任务适应几何与OOD引入的失真；4. 通过受控分布偏移和残差缩放干预提供因果证据。

Result: 1. 任务感知剪枝在ID数据上无收益，但持续提升OOD准确率；2. OOD输入会导致层级范数和成对距离轮廓偏离ID轮廓；3. 剪枝通过移除造成/放大失真的层，使OOD表征向适应分布偏移；4. 该行为在不同模型规模下保持一致。

Conclusion: 任务感知剪枝的本质是识别并移除导致OOD输入几何失真的层，将OOD表征重新对齐到模型的任务适应几何结构。这为理解剪枝为何仅改善OOD性能提供了几何视角的理论解释，并通过因果实验验证了该机制。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [224] [Selective Safety Steering via Value-Filtered Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14746)
*Bat-Sheva Einbinder,Hen Davidov,Yee Whye Teh,Yarin Gal,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于价值过滤的解码时引导方法，在减少对安全响应的不必要干预的同时提高不安全响应的安全性，实现了安全性、有用性和与基础模型相似度之间更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全对齐方法在解码时使用安全奖励修改采样策略，但往往会对本就安全的生成内容进行不必要的干预，从而损害基础模型的有用性、流畅性、风格和连贯性等关键属性。

Method: 提出了一种基于价值的安全标准过滤令牌的测试时引导方法，为错误干预的概率提供了显式界限；通过单一阈值超参数控制该界限，使从业者能够在更高不必要干预率和更好输出安全性之间进行权衡。

Result: 在多个数据集和实验中，该方法优于现有基线，在安全性、有用性和与基础模型相似度之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: 该价值过滤解码方法有效减少了对安全响应的不必要干预，同时提升了对不安全响应的安全保障，为大语言模型的安全对齐提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with human values, their generations may still violate safety constraints. A growing line of work addresses this problem by modifying the model's sampling policy at decoding time using a safety reward. However, existing decoding-time steering methods often intervene unnecessarily, modifying generations that would have been safe under the base model. Such unnecessary interventions are undesirable, as they can distort key properties of the base model such as helpfulness, fluency, style, and coherence. We propose a new test-time steering method designed to reduce such unnecessary interventions while improving the safety of unsafe responses. Our approach filters tokens using a value-based safety criterion and provides an explicit bound on the probability of false interventions. A single threshold hyperparameter controls this bound, allowing practitioners to trade off higher rates of unnecessary intervention for better output safety. Across multiple datasets and experiments, we show that our value-filtered decoding method outperforms existing baselines, achieving better trade-offs between safety, helpfulness, and similarity to the base model.

</details>


### [225] [Cognitive-Uncertainty Guided Knowledge Distillation for Accurate Classification of Student Misconceptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14752)
*Qirui Liu,Hao Chen,Weijie Shi,Jiajie Xu,Jia Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段知识蒸馏框架，通过双层边缘选择机制挖掘高价值样本，在仅使用10.30%的筛选样本和4B参数模型的情况下，显著优于SOTA大模型和标准微调的72B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别学生误解对个性化教育至关重要，但面临三大挑战：(1)数据稀缺且呈长尾分布，真实学生推理难以合成；(2)错误类别边界模糊，标注噪声高；(3)部署悖论——大模型因预训练偏见忽视非传统方法且无法边缘部署，小模型易过拟合噪声。

Method: 提出两阶段知识蒸馏框架。第一阶段进行标准蒸馏迁移任务能力；第二阶段引入基于认知不确定性的双层边缘选择机制，根据教师模型不确定性和置信度差异识别四类关键样本。针对不同数据子集设计难度自适应机制，平衡硬/软标签贡献，使学生模型既能从教师软标签继承类间关系，又能区分模糊错误类型。

Result: 在MAP-Charting数据集上，仅使用10.30%的筛选样本进行增强训练，MAP@3达到0.9585（+17.8%）；在中学代数误解基准的跨主题测试中，仅用4B参数模型达到84.38%准确率，显著优于SOTA大模型（67.73%）和标准微调的72B模型（81.25%）。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了学生误解识别中的数据稀缺、标注噪声和模型部署问题，证明了通过精心设计的选择机制挖掘高价值样本，小规模模型可以超越大规模模型性能，为教育领域的模型部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurately identifying student misconceptions is crucial for personalized education but faces three challenges: (1) data scarcity with long-tail distribution, where authentic student reasoning is difficult to synthesize; (2) fuzzy boundaries between error categories with high annotation noise; (3) deployment parado-large models overlook unconventional approaches due to pretraining bias and cannot be deployed on edge, while small models overfit to noise. Unlike traditional methods that increase diversity through large-scale data synthesis, we propose a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that mines high-value samples from existing data. The first stage performs standard distillation to transfer task capabilities. The second stage introduces a dual-layer marginal selection mechanism based on cognitive uncertainty, identifying four types of critical samples based on teacher model uncertainty and confidence differences. For different data subsets, we design difficulty-adaptive mechanism to balance hard/soft label contributions, enabling student models to inherit inter-class relationships from teacher soft labels while distinguishing ambiguous error types. Experiments show that with augmented training on only 10.30% of filtered samples, we achieve MAP@3 of 0.9585 (+17.8%) on the MAP-Charting dataset, and using only a 4B parameter model, we attain 84.38% accuracy on cross-topic tests of middle school algebra misconception benchmarks, significantly outperforming sota LLM (67.73%) and standard fine-tuned 72B models (81.25%). Our code is available at https://github.com/RoschildRui/acl2026_map.

</details>


### [226] [Crys-JEPA: Accelerating Crystal Discovery via Embedding Screening and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14759)
*Nian Liu,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Yann LeCun,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Crys-JEPA，一种用于晶体生成的联合嵌入预测架构，通过学习能量感知的潜在空间来解决现有模型中稳定性与新颖性之间的权衡问题，在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上V.S.U.N指标分别提升81.4%和82.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晶体生成模型通过最大化观测晶体的似然进行训练，导致样本倾向于接近已知材料，这与材料发现中的稳定性、新颖性标准不一致。实验发现当前模型陷入稳定性-新颖性权衡：靠近观测分布保持稳定但限制新颖性，远离则快速丧失稳定性，导致既能稳定又新颖的晶体发现区域极其狭窄。

Method: 提出Crys-JEPA（晶体的联合嵌入预测架构），学习保持形成能差异的能量感知潜在空间。在此空间中，稳定性评估可重构为与可访问训练晶体的嵌入比较，减少对昂贵的能量评估和特定任务外部参考的依赖。在此基础上构建筛选-优化流水线，识别有前景的生成晶体并将其重新引入以优化生成模型。

Result: 在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上，Crys-JEPA在V.S.U.N（Valid, Stable, Unique, Novel）指标上分别相比基线模型提升81.4%和82.6%。

Conclusion: Crys-JEPA通过构建能量感知潜在空间成功缓解了晶体生成中的稳定性-新颖性权衡问题，筛选-优化流水线进一步提升了生成质量，为发现既稳定又新颖的晶体材料提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation seeks to discover materials that are not merely realistic, but also stable and novel. However, most existing generative models are trained to maximize the likelihood of observed crystals, which encourages samples to stay close to known materials yet not necessarily align with the criteria that matter in discovery. Through an empirical investigation, we show that current crystal generative models are caught in a pronounced stability--novelty trade-off: moving toward the observed distribution preserves stability but limits novelty, whereas moving away from it quickly destroys stability. This suggests that the useful region for discovering crystals that are both stable and novel is extremely narrow. To escape the trade-off, we introduce Crys-JEPA, a joint embedding predictive architecture for crystals that learns an energy-aware latent space preserving formation-energy differences. In this space, stability assessment can be reformulated as an embedding-based comparison against accessible training crystals, reducing the reliance on expensive energy evaluation and task-specific external references. Building on Crys-JEPA, we further develop a screening-and-refinement pipeline that identifies promising generated crystals and reintroduces them to refine the generative model. On MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 datasets, we achieve improvements over baselines up to 81.4% and 82.6% on V.S.U.N metric, respectively.

</details>


### [227] [Compositional Sparsity as an Inductive Bias for Neural Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14764)
*Hongyu Lin,Antonio Briola,Yuanrong Wang,Tomaso Aste*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合信息过滤网络(IFNs)和同调神经网络(HNNs)的方法，通过提取稀疏依赖结构并将其映射为固定连线的稀疏神经图，成功解决了深度神经网络在高维学习中的维数灾难问题。HNNs在参数量大幅减少的情况下，性能匹敌或超越稠密基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别使深度神经网络(DNNs)能够克服维数灾难的结构先验是机器学习理论中的一个基本挑战。现有文献表明，有效的高维学习由组合稀疏性驱动，即目标函数分解为由低维变量子集支持的组成成分。作者旨在验证这一假设，并探索如何利用稀疏依赖结构来构建更高效的神经网络。

Method: 作者结合了两种技术：(1)信息过滤网络(IFNs)，通过约束信息最大化提取稀疏依赖结构；(2)同调神经网络(HNNs)，将推断出的拓扑结构映射为固定连线的稀疏神经图。该方法形式化了潜在的设计原则，提出了一个可解释的管道，其中抽象通过层次组合而涌现。HNNs比标准DNNs稀疏数个数量级，且仅需最少的超参数调整。

Result: 在具有已知稀疏层次结构的合成任务中，HNNs恢复了底层的组合结构，并在维数增加时稠密替代方案性能退化的情况下保持稳定。在广泛的现实世界数据集上，HNNs在使用远更少参数的情况下，始终匹配或超越稠密基线，表现出更低的方差和对超参数的敏感性降低。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持了组合稀疏性是使DNNs克服维数灾难的关键结构先验这一假设。通过显式地编码稀疏依赖结构，HNNs提供了一种可解释、高效且稳定的神经网络架构，为理解深度学习的成功机制提供了新视角，并为构建更高效的神经网络系统提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Identifying the structural priors that enable Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) to overcome the curse of dimensionality is a fundamental challenge in machine learning theory. Existing literature suggests that effective high-dimensional learning is driven by compositional sparsity, where target functions decompose into constituents supported on low-dimensional variable subsets. To investigate this hypothesis, we combine Information Filtering Networks (IFNs), which extract sparse dependency structures via constrained information maximisation, with Homological Neural Networks (HNNs), which map the inferred topology into fixed-wiring sparse neural graphs. We formalise the design principles underlying this construction and present an interpretable pipeline in which abstraction emerges through hierarchical composition. HNNs are orders of magnitude sparser than standard DNNs and require only minimal hyperparameter tuning. On synthetic tasks with known sparse hierarchies, HNNs recover the underlying compositional structure and remain stable in regimes where dense alternatives degrade as dimensionality increases. Across a broad suite of real-world datasets, HNNs consistently match or outperform dense baselines while using far fewer parameters, exhibiting lower variance and showing reduced sensitivity to hyperparameters.

</details>


### [228] [Composable Crystals: Controllable Materials Discovery via Concept Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14769)
*Nian Liu,Yuwei Zeng,Ryoji Kubo,Nikita Kazeev,Stephen Gregory Dale,Artem Maevskiy,Pengru Huang,Thomas Laurent,Kostya S. Novoselov,Xavier Bresson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于概念的晶体生成框架，通过学习可解释的晶体概念并重新组合，实现了可控的超越训练分布的新型晶体生成，在V.S.U.N指标上相比基础模型提升了约50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的晶体生成方法主要依赖黑盒随机采样，对生成结构如何超越观测分布缺乏控制，难以有效探索新型材料空间。因此需要一种可解释、可控的方法来引导生成过程。

Method: 训练一个向量量化变分自编码器(VQ-VAE)自动发现可重用的晶体概念作为构建块，这些概念从局部原子环境和全局对称模式中学习得到。通过重组这些概念进行可控生成，并引入组合生成器利用模型自生成的高质量样本进行迭代优化。

Result: 在MP-20和Alex-MP-20数据集上的实验表明，概念组合方法在V.S.U.N指标上分别使基础模型提升53.2%和51.7%，特别是在新颖性方面取得显著进步。学习到的概念具有良好的可解释性，并能泛化到不同分布的晶体。

Conclusion: 该概念组合框架成功实现了可控的晶体生成，能够有效探索训练分布之外的新型晶体结构，为材料发现提供了一种新的可解释、可控制的生成范式。

Abstract: De novo crystal generation, a central task in materials discovery, aims to generate crystals that are simultaneously valid, stable, unique, and novel. Existing methods mainly rely on black-box stochastic sampling, providing limited control over how generated structures move beyond the observed distribution. In this paper, we introduce a concept-based compositional framework for crystal generation. We train a vector-quantized variational autoencoder to automatically discover a shared set of reusable crystal concepts, which serve as building blocks for guided generation. These learned concepts naturally exhibit interpretability from both local atomic environments and global symmetry patterns, and generalize to crystals from different distributions. By recombining such concepts, our framework enables controllable exploration of novel crystals beyond the training distribution, rather than relying solely on unconstrained random sampling. To further improve composition efficiency, we introduce a composition generator and iteratively refine it using high-quality samples generated by the model itself. The resulting concept compositions are then used to condition downstream crystal generation. Numerical experiments on MP-20 and Alex-MP-20 show that compositing concepts separately increase base model up to 53.2% and 51.7% on V.S.U.N metric, with particular gains in novelty.

</details>


### [229] [Beyond What to Select: A Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-Volume Scheduling for Efficient Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14773)
*Suorong Yang,Hanqi Zhu,Hai Gan,Fangjian Su,Guang Li,Furao Shen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PODS框架，通过动态调度数据选择量而非设计新的样本评分标准，在训练过程中交替使用低选择比率的正则化阶段和高选择比率的恢复阶段，从而在保持模型性能的同时显著降低训练成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据选择方法主要关注样本重要性标准的设计，但在训练过程中通常固定选择数据量，导致样本身份动态变化但数据量静态不变。作者从优化角度重新审视数据选择，发现选定数据训练会产生由瞬时选择比率调节的隐式正则化效应，揭示了低比率增强正则化与高比率保持数据覆盖和优化保真度之间的关键权衡。

Method: 提出PODS（Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling）框架，这是一个轻量级、比率级别、任务无关的即插即用模块。PODS在目标选择比率下，动态调度训练过程中选择的数据量，交替执行低比率正则化阶段和高比率恢复阶段，在不牺牲优化稳定性的前提下利用选择诱导的正则化效应。该方法兼容现有的静态和动态选择方法。

Result: 在多个数据集、架构和任务上的实验表明，PODS持续改善效率-泛化权衡。具体而言，在ImageNet-1k上将训练成本降低50%并提升准确率；在大语言模型指令微调任务上实现超过2倍的加速且无性能下降。

Conclusion: PODS通过动态调度数据选择量而非改进样本评分，成功利用了数据选择诱导的隐式正则化效应。其轻量级、即插即用的设计使其能够广泛应用于现有数据选择方法和不同的训练范式，在提升训练效率的同时保持甚至改善模型性能。

Abstract: Data selection accelerates training by identifying representative training data while preserving model performance. However, existing methods mainly focus on designing sample-importance criteria, i.e., deciding what to select, while typically fixing the selected data volume as the target ratio throughout training. Thus, they are often dynamic in sample identity but static in data volume. In this work, we revisit data selection from an optimization perspective and show that selected-data training induces an implicit regularization effect modulated by the instantaneous selection ratio. This reveals a key trade-off: lower ratios amplify selection-induced regularization, whereas higher ratios preserve data coverage and optimization fidelity. Motivated by this insight, we propose PODS, a Plug-and-play Oscillatory Data-volume Scheduling framework. Rather than introducing another sample-scoring metric, PODS serves as a lightweight module that dynamically schedules how much data to select over training. Under the target selection ratio, PODS alternates between low-ratio regularization phases and high-ratio recovery phases to exploit selection-induced regularization without sacrificing optimization stability. With its lightweight, ratio-level, and task-agnostic design, PODS is compatible with existing static and dynamic selection methods and broadly applicable across training paradigms. Experiments across various datasets, architectures, and tasks show that PODS consistently improves the efficiency-generalization trade-off, e.g., reducing ImageNet-1k training cost by 50% with improved accuracy and accelerating LLM instruction tuning by over 2x without performance degradation.

</details>


### [230] [Peng's Q($λ$) for Conservative Value Estimation in Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14779)
*Byeongchan Kim,Min-hwan Oh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了保守Peng's Q(λ) (CPQL)算法，这是首个在离线强化学习中成功应用多步算子进行保守价值估计的方法，在D4RL基准上显著优于现有单步基线，并能促进离线到在线学习的平稳过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习中的保守方法存在两个关键问题：一是大多采用单步Bellman算子，未能充分利用离线轨迹中的多步信息；二是难以在缓解过度悲观价值估计的同时，保证策略性能不低于行为策略并接近最优性能。

Method: 提出Conservative Peng's Q(λ) (CPQL)算法，该方法将Peng's Q(λ) (PQL)算子适配用于保守价值估计以替代Bellman算子。PQL算子的不动点在离线RL中更接近行为策略的价值函数，从而自然地引入隐式行为正则化，在减轻过度悲观估计的同时提供近似最优性能保证。

Result: 在D4RL基准上的大量数值实验表明，CPQL持续且显著地优于现有的离线单步基线方法。在离线到在线学习框架中，使用CPQL预训练的Q函数可以使在线PQL智能体避免微调开始时通常出现的性能下降，并获得稳健的性能提升。

Conclusion: CPQL是首个在理论和实证上证明多步算子用于保守价值估计在离线RL中有效性的工作，实现了同时缓解过度悲观估计、保证不低于行为策略性能、提供近似最优性能保证这三个此前保守方法无法同时达成的重要里程碑，并对离线到在线学习框架做出了贡献。

Abstract: We propose a model-free offline multi-step reinforcement learning (RL) algorithm, Conservative Peng's Q($λ$) (CPQL). Our algorithm adapts the Peng's Q($λ$) (PQL) operator for conservative value estimation as an alternative to the Bellman operator. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work in offline RL to theoretically and empirically demonstrate the effectiveness of conservative value estimation with a \textit{multi-step} operator by fully leveraging offline trajectories. The fixed point of the PQL operator in offline RL lies closer to the value function of the behavior policy, thereby naturally inducing implicit behavior regularization. CPQL simultaneously mitigates over-pessimistic value estimation, achieves performance greater than (or equal to) that of the behavior policy, and provides near-optimal performance guarantees -- a milestone that previous conservative approaches could not achieve. Extensive numerical experiments on the D4RL benchmark demonstrate that CPQL consistently and significantly outperforms existing offline single-step baselines. In addition to the contributions of CPQL in offline RL, our proposed method also contributes to the offline-to-online learning framework. Using the Q-function pre-trained by CPQL in offline settings enables the online PQL agent to avoid the performance drop typically observed at the start of fine-tuning and to attain robust performance improvements. Our code is available at https://github.com/oh-lab/CPQL.

</details>


### [231] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于回放的类增量学习（CIL）中存在系统性且严重的“不平衡遗忘”现象（即某些类别遗忘程度远高于其他类别），并通过构建三个最后一层系数成功预测了这种遗忘的排名，发现“自诱导干扰”是导致该现象的最强预测因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管回放策略被广泛用于缓解类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘，但近期研究发现，即使平衡分配回放样本，不同类别间的遗忘程度仍存在显著差异。这种“不平衡遗忘”现象具有重要研究价值，但目前尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在系统性地研究该现象的成因与机制。

Method: 作者从原理性分析出发，构建了三个最后一层系数，用于捕捉在增量学习步骤中影响每个过去类别的不同梯度级干扰源。随后通过实验验证这些系数能否可靠地预测各类别在步骤结束时的遗忘排名。此外，通过控制实验分析各系数间的因果关系。

Result: 实验结果表明：1）不平衡遗忘现象在基于回放的CIL中系统且严重地存在；2）所提出的三个系数联合起来能够可靠预测各类别的遗忘排名；3）“自诱导干扰”系数是最强的预测因子；4）控制实验提供了证据，表明该系数受“新类干扰系数”的影响。这些结果支持将系数作为连接训练期间梯度级交互与类别级遗忘结果的合理机制解释。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解不平衡遗忘现象提供了重要的机制性见解，揭示了最后一层梯度级干扰源与类别级遗忘结果之间的关联。研究建议，通过减少已识别干扰源在类别间的差异，是缓解不平衡遗忘的有前景的方向。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [232] [GFMate: Empowering Graph Foundation Models with Test-time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14809)
*Yan Jiang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GFMate框架，通过预训练无关的测试时图提示调优方法，解决了现有方法中提示与特定源域纠缠、泛化能力差且忽视未标记目标域数据的问题，在12个基准数据集上实现了最高30.63%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图提示学习方法存在两大局限：(1)提示与特定源域信息和GFM预训练策略纠缠，限制了其在不同域和不同GFMs间的泛化能力；(2)仅依赖少样本调优进行适应，忽视了目标域测试数据中丰富的未标记信息。

Method: 提出GFMate框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)质心提示和层提示，在目标域预训练后应用，避免与特定源域和模型预训练过程纠缠；(2)设计测试时互补学习目标，同时利用标记和未标记的目标域数据进行有效的测试时提示调优。

Result: 在12个基准数据集上进行广泛实验，证明了GFMate的优越性能和效率，实现了最高30.63%的性能提升。

Conclusion: GFMate成功实现了预训练无关的测试时图提示调优，通过解耦提示与源域及预训练策略的依赖，并充分利用目标域的标记和未标记数据，显著提升了图基础模型的跨域泛化能力和性能表现。

Abstract: Graph prompt tuning has shown great potential in graph learning by introducing trainable prompts to enhance the model performance in conventional single-domain scenarios. Recent research has extended graph prompts to improve Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) by few-shot tuning auxiliary prompts. Despite their progress, most existing methods embed source-domain information into prompts, which serve either as input to GFMs or encoded during model pre-training. Such prompt entanglement with specific source domains and GFM pre-training strategy restricts their generalisability to other domains and different GFMs. Furthermore, existing GFM prompts merely rely on few-shot tuning for adaptation, neglecting the rich information in unlabelled target domain test data. Motivated by these insights, this paper aims to empower GFMs with pre-training-agnostic test-time graph prompt tuning, named GFMate. GFMate introduces centroid and layer prompts applied after pre-training on target domains, avoiding entanglement with specific source domains and model pre-training. In addition, a test-time complementary learning objective is devised to exploit both labelled and unlabelled target domain data for effective test-time prompt tuning. Extensive experiments on 12 benchmark datasets demonstrate the superior performance and efficiency of GFMate, achieving improvements of up to 30.63%. Code is available at https://github.com/YanJiangJerry/GFMate.

</details>


### [233] [ToMAToMP: Robust and Multi-Parameter Topological Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14824)
*Ludo Andrianirina,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ToMAToMP，这是首个能够同时处理多个函数并具有理论保证的拓扑聚类方法，解决了原ToMATo算法对图参数敏感、易受异常值影响以及仅支持单函数的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的拓扑聚类算法ToMATo虽然具有通用性和鲁棒性保证，但存在三个主要缺陷：需要用户手动调节图超参数、对函数值中的异常值敏感、以及仅能处理单一函数而实际应用常需多函数分析。这些限制促使作者开发更通用的多参数拓扑聚类方法。

Method: 利用多参数持续同调中的MMA分解工具设计ToMAToMP算法，使其能够同时处理多个用户定义函数。该方法将多函数聚类问题转化为多参数拓扑数据分析框架，并提供了完整的理论推导和鲁棒性证明。

Result: ToMAToMP在多个数据集上的数值实验表明，相比非拓扑和拓扑聚类基线方法，该方法在聚类质量和效率方面均有显著提升。理论分析证明该方法具有鲁棒性保证，且作为推论可解决图参数调节和异常值敏感性问题。

Conclusion: ToMAToMP成功将拓扑聚类扩展到多参数场景，不仅实现了多函数同时处理，还解决了原算法的参数敏感性及鲁棒性问题，为拓扑数据分析在复杂聚类任务中的应用提供了新的理论工具和实践方法。

Abstract: Topological clustering, and its main algorithm ToMATo, is a clustering method from Topological Data Analysis (TDA) which has been applied successfully in several applications during the last few years. This is due to its high versatility, as clusters are detected from the persistent components in the sublevel sets of any user-defined function (gene expression, pixel values, etc), and efficiency, as topological clustering enjoys robustness guarantees. However, ToMATo is also limited in several ways. First, a graph on the data points needs to be provided as a hyper-parameter of the method (whose fine-tuning is left to the user). Second, ToMATo is known to be very sensitive to outlier values in the function range. Finally, and most importantly, ToMATo can only handle one function at a time, whereas it is critical to use several functions in various applications. In this article, we introduce ToMAToMP: the first topological clustering method able to handle several functions at the same time with theoretical guarantees. More specifically, we leverage a recent tool from multi-parameter persistent homology, called MMA decomposition, to design our clustering algorithm, and prove that it enjoys robustness properties. As corollaries, we show that it can be used to make ToMATo independent of graph tuning, and robust to outliers. Finally, we provide a set of numerical experiments showcasing the efficiency and quality of the clusterings produced by ToMAToMP, by showing strong improvement over non-topological and topological baselines for various datasets.

</details>


### [234] [GenAI for Energy-Efficient and Interference-Aware Compressed Sensing of GNSS Signals on a Google Edge TPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14839)
*Thorben Wegner,Lucas Heublein,Tobias Feigl,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Alexander Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式AI（VAE）的方法，在Google Edge TPU上实现GNSS干扰信号的实时压缩与分类，在边缘端实现>42倍压缩率和72类干扰的高精度分类（F2-score 0.915）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS干扰分类方法依赖后处理原始或频谱数据，需向云端传输大量数据，成本高、延迟大，难以满足干扰日益普遍场景下对实时、低功耗解决方案的迫切需求。

Method: 研究多种自编码器架构对GNSS信号进行压缩与重建，采用8-bit量化将大规模模型部署至Google Edge TPU，在接收机硬件端直接处理原始IQ数据、FFT数据及手工特征；同时引入条件VAE与FactorVAE进行消融研究，探索潜特征解耦以提升可解释性。

Result: 系统实现>42倍信号压缩；在重建信号上对约72类干扰进行分类，F2-score达0.915，接近原始信号的0.923；大幅降低干扰信号传输成本，适合功耗受限的边缘部署环境。

Conclusion: 该硬件导向的GenAI方案有效解决了GNSS干扰信号实时分类与带宽瓶颈问题，为敏感干扰应用提供可解释、可信且低成本的实用解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional methods for classifying global navigation satellite system (GNSS) jamming signals typically involve post-processing raw or spectral data streams, requiring complex and costly data transmission to cloud-based interference classification systems. In contrast, our proposed approach efficiently compresses GNSS data streams directly at the hardware receiver while simultaneously classifying jamming and spoofing attacks in real time. Given the growing prevalence of GNSS jamming, there is a critical need for real-time solutions suitable for power-constrained environments. This paper introduces a novel method for compressing and classifying GNSS jamming threats using generative artificial intelligence (GenAI), specifically variational autoencoders (VAEs), deployed on Google Edge tensor processing units (TPUs). The study evaluates various autoencoder (AE) architectures to compress and reconstruct GNSS signals, focusing on preserving interference characteristics while minimizing data size near the receiver hardware. The pipeline adapts large-scale AE models for Google Edge TPUs through 8-bit quantization to ensure energy-efficient deployment. Tests on raw in-phase and quadrature-phase (IQ) data, Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) data, and handcrafted features show the system achieves significant compression (>42x) and accurate classification of approximately 72 interference types on reconstructed signals (F2-score 0.915), closely matching the original signals (F2-score 0.923). The hardware-centric GenAI approach also substantially reduces jammer signal transmission costs, offering a practical solution for interference mitigation. Ablation studies on conditional and factorized VAEs (i.e., FactorVAE) explore latent feature disentanglement for data generation, enhancing model interpretability and fostering trust in machine learning (ML) solutions for sensitive interference applications.

</details>


### [235] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对决策依赖审查的库存控制问题（如重复报童模型），提出了上下文生成后验采样（ICGPS）方法。该方法结合离线生成模型与在线决策，在理论和实验上均表现出色，尤其在先验失配和分布偏移场景下具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理决策依赖审查的方法存在局限：基于参数化Thompson采样（TS）的方法在先验失配时表现脆弱，而离线插值方法难以直接迁移到在线学习场景。受决策预测视角启发，作者旨在结合两类方法的优势，通过在学习的潜在需求补全上采取神谕行动来解决此问题。

Method: 提出上下文生成后验采样（ICGPS）框架：利用离线元训练的现代生成模型，通过上下文自回归生成进行在线部署。具体实现为ChronosFlow，结合冻结的时间序列Transformer主干与可训练的条件归一化流头，实现快速的审查一致性采样。核心思想是在学习的补全核上采取行动，将审查反馈问题转化为凸优化反馈问题。

Result: 理论表明ICGPS的贝叶斯遗憾受限于具有理想补全核的TS基准的贝叶斯遗憾，加上一个与部署惩罚相关的项（尺度为√T乘以补全失配的平方根）。对于重复报童问题推导出了次线性贝叶斯遗憾。在合理覆盖和稳定性假设下，离线预测质量可迁移至在线性能。实验中，ChronosFlow-ICGPS与正确设定的TS性能相当，优于短视和UCN基线方法，对先验失配和分布偏移具有鲁棒性，在真实SuperStore数据集上表现优异。

Conclusion: ICGPS成功地将离线生成模型的能力迁移至在线决策场景，为决策依赖审查问题提供了有效的解决方案。该方法不仅具有理论保证，还在实际应用中展现出优越性能和鲁棒性，为运营管理问题提供了通用的插件模板。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation. Theoretically, we show that the Bayesian regret of ICGPS with a learned completion kernel is bounded by the Bayesian regret of a TS benchmark with the ideal completion kernel plus a deployment penalty scaling as $\sqrt{T}$ times the square root of the completion mismatch. This yields a plug-in template for operational problems with known TS regret bounds. For R-NV, we derive sublinear Bayesian regret by reducing censored feedback to bandit convex optimization feedback. We also show that, under reasonable coverage and stability assumptions, the online completion mismatch is controlled by the offline censored predictive mismatch, so offline predictive quality transfers to online performance. Practically, we instantiate ICGPS with ChronosFlow, which combines a frozen time-series transformer backbone with a trainable conditional normalizing-flow head for fast censoring-consistent sampling. In benchmark experiments, ChronosFlow-ICGPS matches correctly specified TS, outperforms myopic and UCB-style baselines, and is robust to prior mismatch and distribution shift. ChronosFlow-ICGPS also performs well for the real-world SuperStore dataset, especially under heavy censoring.

</details>


### [236] [GPart: End-to-End Isometric Fine-Tuning via Global Parameter Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14841)
*Paolo Mandica,Michał Brzozowski,Zuzanna Dubanowska,Neo Christopher Chung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GPart，一种通过单一等距划分矩阵将低维向量直接映射到全权重空间的参数高效微调方法，解决了LoRA双线性结构破坏距离保持的问题，在极低存储成本下实现了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流的LoRA方法因其双线性结构导致参数到权重更新的映射不保持距离，扭曲了优化景观；改进方法如Uni-LoRA虽然提升了参数效率，但仍无法解决端到端等距性缺失的核心问题。

Method: 提出GPart（Global Partition fine-tuning），使用单一等距划分矩阵将d维可训练向量直接映射到模型的全权重空间。该方法基于有效微调可从全权重空间的随机低维子空间中产生的理论前提，完全移除了低秩矩阵结构约束，仅需一个随机投影、一个超参数(d)，存储成本仅为d+1个值。

Result: 在自然语言理解、计算机视觉任务和数学推理任务上的实验表明，GPart相比现有PEFT方法实现了优越或相当的性能，达到了最先进的效率和性能平衡。

Conclusion: GPart通过移除结构约束实现了极致的参数效率和简化流程，为参数高效微调提供了一条简洁优雅的路径，证明了在全权重空间的随机低维子空间中进行微调的有效性。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has become the dominant paradigm for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of large language models (LLMs). However, its bilinear structure introduces a critical limitation: the mapping from trainable parameters to weight updates is not distance-preserving, distorting the optimization landscape. Methods that project a low-dimensional vector into LoRA's parameter space, such as Uni-LoRA, improve parameter efficiency, but the subsequent bilinear LoRA map breaks end-to-end isometry, leaving the core distance-preservation problem unresolved. We propose GPart (Global Partition fine-tuning), a highly parameter-efficient fine-tuning method which removes the low-rank bottleneck entirely. Our method uses a single isometric partition matrix to map a $d$-dimensional trainable vector directly into the full weight space of the model. The result is an extremely minimal fine-tuning pipeline: one random projection, end-to-end isometric, with a single clean hyperparameter ($d$) and storage cost of $d+1$ values (the trainable vector plus a random seed). GPart builds on the theoretical premise that effective fine-tuning can emerge from random low-dimensional subspaces of the full weight space, without imposing low-rank matrix structure. We empirically demonstrate the superior or comparable performance of GPart to existing PEFT methods on natural language understanding, computer vision tasks, and mathematical reasoning. Overall, GPart achieves state-of-the-art efficiency and performance by removing structural constraints, offering a straightforward and elegant path to PEFT.

</details>


### [237] [XFP: Quality-Targeted Adaptive Codebook Quantization with Sparse Outlier Separation for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14844)
*Thomas Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XFP，一种LLM推理的动态权重量化器，通过设定每通道余弦相似度阈值自动确定量化参数，无需校准数据或手动选择位宽。在Qwen3.5-122B上实现138 tok/s解码速度，GSM8K准确率94.49%；针对超大模型提出H-Process流程，使Qwen3.5-397B在有限显存下实现100.9 tok/s解码，准确率66.72%，在内存、吞吐量和精度上均超越INT4基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM量化方法通常需要Hessian信息、校准数据或手动设置位宽，工作流程复杂且缺乏灵活性。面对不同硬件的内存限制，现有方法难以自动平衡模型质量与存储需求，尤其是对于MoE等超大模型，迫切需要一种能根据质量要求自动确定量化参数的动态量化方案。

Method: XFP反转传统量化工作流：操作者指定每通道余弦相似度的重建质量下限（注意力层和共享专家设严格阈值，路由专家MoE设宽松阈值），系统自动确定码本大小、离群值预算和每层打包方式。每个权重矩阵分解为稀疏fp16离群值残差和密集的子字节索引张量（指向分组学习码本）。提供两种存储模式V2（每通道Lloyd算法）和V2a（每层共享32个码本库），共享自动选择前端和融合解码内核。针对超出目标内存的模型，提出H-Process流程：基于两个余弦阈值进行质量驱动的迭代搜索，在OOM边界和生成质量边界约束下找到模型刚好适配的运行点。

Result: 在Qwen3.5-122B-A10B上，V2模式单流解码达138 tok/s（RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell，TP=2），GSM8K严格匹配率94.49%，TP=1时比Marlin INT4快49%。在Qwen3.5-397B-A17B（每层512个路由专家）上，H-Process将完整专家群体压缩至约3.4有效位宽，适配2x96 GB显存，长输出解码速度100.9 tok/s，GSM8K严格匹配率66.72%（全1319题集），在内存占用、吞吐量和精度三个维度同时超越带路由专家剪枝的INT4方案。

Conclusion: XFP通过反转量化工作流实现了无需校准数据的自动化量化，在保持高精度的同时显著提升推理速度。H-Process为超大模型在有限硬件资源下的部署提供了系统化解决方案，证明了在内存、吞吐量和精度三者间可同时取得优于传统INT4方法的效果，为LLM高效部署开辟了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce XFP, a dynamic weight quantizer for LLM inference that inverts the conventional workflow: the operator specifies reconstruction quality floors on per-channel cosine similarity (one strict floor for attention and shared experts, one lazy floor for routed-expert MoE); XFP determines codebook size, outlier budget, and packing per layer automatically -- no Hessian, no calibration data, no manual bit-width selection. Each weight matrix is decomposed into a sparse fp16 outlier residual and a dense sub-byte index tensor into a per-group learned codebook. Two storage modes share one auto-select frontend and one fused decode kernel: V2 (per-channel Lloyd) and V2a (shared library of L=32 codebooks per layer). On Qwen3.5-122B-A10B under V2, XFP reaches 138 tok/s single-stream decode on workstation hardware (RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, TP=2) at 94.49% GSM8K strict-match (3 seeds, n=3957), and is 49% faster than Marlin INT4 at TP=1. For models that do not fit in the target memory envelope, we present the H-Process: a quality-driven iteration over the two cosine thresholds that finds the operating point at which the model just fits while still producing sensible output. Three constraints define its search space: the operator-set thresholds, an OOM boundary at quantize-on-load, and a garbage boundary in generation (cosine similarity steers; benches verify). On Qwen3.5-397B-A17B (512 routed experts/layer), the H-Process fits the full expert population into 2x96 GB at ~3.4 effective bits and delivers 100.9 tok/s long-output decode at 66.72% GSM8K strict-match on the full 1319-problem set (single seed at submission; multi-seed evaluation in progress), exceeding INT4 with routed-expert pruning on memory, throughput, and accuracy simultaneously.

</details>


### [238] [Exploitation of Hidden Context in Dynamic Movement Forecasting: A Neural Network Journey from Recurrent to Graph Neural Networks and General Purpose Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14855)
*Lukas Schelenz,Shobha Rajanna,Denis Gosalci,Lucas Heublein,Jonas Pirkl,Jonathan Ott,Felix Ott,Christopher Mutschler,Tobias Feigl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种轨迹预测模型在NBA球员动态运动预测任务中的表现。基于机器学习的方法在2秒预测范围内显著优于传统线性模型，其中融合上下文信息的混合LSTM模型取得了最低的最终位移误差（FDE）1.51米，且相比图注意力网络（GAT）和Transformer需要更少的训练数据和训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在信号处理流程中，轨迹预测对于缓解延迟至关重要，尤其是在预测NBA球员等动态物体的运动时。由于体育运动具有内在的交互性和不可预测性，速度和方向的突变非常普遍，传统方法难以有效建模其中的非线性动力学。现有的机器学习方法虽然提供了更高的灵活性和准确性，但往往无法显式捕捉时间依赖性与上下文交互之间的相互作用，而这些因素在混乱的体育环境中至关重要。因此，需要对这些模型进行全面评估，明确其优缺点。

Method: 本文系统评估了传统方法（(S)ARIMA(X)、卡尔曼滤波器、粒子滤波器）与机器学习方法（LSTM、图神经网络、Transformer、时间卷积神经网络TCNN、图注意力网络GAT）在NBA球员轨迹预测任务中的表现。研究重点分析了输入历史长度、泛化能力以及融合上下文信息的能力对模型性能的影响。作者提出了一种融合上下文信息的混合LSTM架构，旨在更好地捕捉时间依赖性与上下文交互之间的关系。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于机器学习的方法在长达2秒的预测范围内，相比线性模型展现出显著改进。在所有测试架构中，融合上下文信息的混合LSTM模型表现最优，取得了最低的最终位移误差（FDE）1.51米，超越了TCNN、GAT和Transformer模型。此外，该混合LSTM模型相比GAT和Transformer需要更少的训练数据和训练时间。研究还发现，不同模型在输入历史长度和泛化能力上存在关键的性能权衡，没有单一架构能在所有指标上表现卓越。

Conclusion: 在快节奏、动态环境（如NBA比赛）的轨迹预测任务中，不存在单一的最优架构，各模型在不同评估维度上呈现出性能权衡。因此，在实际应用中需要根据具体任务需求进行模型选择和设计，特别是需要考虑输入历史长度、泛化能力以及上下文信息的融合方式。融合上下文信息的混合LSTM模型在准确性、数据效率和训练效率方面取得了良好的平衡，是一种具有实用价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Forecasting within signal processing pipelines is crucial for mitigating delays, particularly in predicting the dynamic movements of objects such as NBA players. This task poses significant challenges due to the inherently interactive and unpredictable nature of sports, where abrupt changes in velocity and direction are prevalent. Traditional approaches, including (S)ARIMA(X), Kalman filters (KF), and Particle filters (PF), often struggle to model the non-linear dynamics present in such scenarios. Machine learning (ML) methods, such as long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, graph neural networks (GNNs), and Transformers, offer greater flexibility and accuracy but frequently fail to explicitly capture the interplay between temporal dependencies and contextual interactions, which are critical in chaotic sports environments. In this paper, we evaluate these models and assess their strengths and weaknesses. Experimental results reveal key performance trade-offs across input history length, generalizability, and the ability to incorporate contextual information. ML-based methods demonstrated substantial improvements over linear models across forecast horizons of up to 2s. Among the tested architectures, our hybrid LSTM augmented with contextual information achieved the lowest final displacement error (FDE) of 1.51m, outperforming temporal convolutional neural network (TCNN), graph attention network (GAT), and Transformers, while also requiring less data and training time compared to GAT and Transformers. Our findings indicate that no single architecture excels across all metrics, emphasizing the need for task-specific considerations in trajectory prediction for fast-paced, dynamic environments such as NBA gameplay.

</details>


### [239] [REALM: Retrospective Encoder Alignment for LFP Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14867)
*Peicheng Wu,Zhenyu Bu,Runze Ma,Lin Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REALM框架，通过回顾性蒸馏方法将离线预训练的双向LFP模型知识迁移到因果模型中，实现了高性能的实时LFP行为解码，参数减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍，为下一代无线植入式BCI提供了可行的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着脑机接口向高通道数和无线操作发展，锋电位信号的高采样频率导致高功耗和带宽需求成为瓶颈。局部场电位(LFP)具有长期稳定性好、能耗低、带宽要求低等优势，但现有的LFP解码模型精度较低且多采用非因果架构，无法满足实时部署需求。

Method: 受语音识别中离线到在线蒸馏策略启发，提出REALM回顾性蒸馏框架：首先使用掩码自编码目标预训练双向Mamba-2教师模型，然后通过表示对齐和任务监督的组合目标将教师模型蒸馏为紧凑的因果学生模型，实现实时部署。

Result: REALM在行为解码任务上持续优于因果和非因果的LFP最先进方法；相比基线方法实现参数量减少2倍、训练时间减少10倍；证明了仅使用LFP的模型可达到竞争性解码性能，无需依赖锋电位信号。

Conclusion: 回顾性蒸馏有效弥合了离线与实时神经解码之间的差距，LFP模型可作为下一代无线植入式BCI的实用、可扩展替代方案，在保证解码性能的同时解决了锋电位信号的功耗和带宽瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Spike activity has been the dominant neural signal for behavior decoding due to its high spatial and temporal resolution. However, as brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) move toward high channel counts and wireless operation, the high sampling frequency of spike signals becomes a bottleneck due to high power and bandwidth requirements. Local field potentials (LFPs) represent a different spatial-temporal scale of brain activity compared to spikes, offering key advantages including improved long-term stability, reduced energy consumption, and lower bandwidth requirement. Despite these benefits, LFP-based decoding models typically show reduced accuracy and often rely on non-causal architectures that are unsuitable for real-time deployment. To address these challenges, we propose REALM: a retrospective distillation framework that enables causal LFP decoding. Inspired by offline-to-online distillation strategies in speech recognition, REALM transfers representational knowledge from a pretrained multi-session bidirectional LFP model to a causal version for real-time deployment. We first pretrain a bidirectional Mamba-2 teacher model using a masked autoencoding objective. We then distill this teacher model into a compact student model via a combined objective of representation alignment and task supervision. REALM consistently outperforms both causal and non-causal LFP-based SOTA methods for behavior decoding. Notably, our REALM improves decoding performance while achieving a $2\times$ reduction in parameter count and a $10\times$ reduction in training time. These results demonstrate that retrospective distillation effectively bridges the gap between offline and real-time neural decoding. REALM shows that LFP-only models can achieve competitive decoding performance without reliance on spike signals, offering a practical and scalable alternative for next-generation wireless implantable BCIs.

</details>


### [240] [Fast Adversarial Attacks with Gradient Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14868)
*Kamil Ciosek,Aleksandr V. Petrov,Nicolò Felicioni,Konstantina Palla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过预测输入梯度来消除反向传播的对抗样本生成方法，在保持较高攻击性能的同时将吞吐量提升了532%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗样本生成方法（即使是快速的FGSM）仍受限于反向传播的计算成本，难以实现大规模高效生成，因此需要一种能够避免反向传播的快速攻击方法。

Method: 提出一系列攻击方法，利用轻量级线性回归从前向传播的隐藏状态预测输入梯度，从而完全消除反向传播过程；该方法基于神经网络的核视角，在神经正切核（NTK）机制下是精确的，且在实际有限宽度模型上依然有效。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在仅使用很小部分时间的情况下恢复了FGSM大部分的攻击性能，吞吐量提升达532%。

Conclusion: 梯度预测是实现在实际时间约束下显著加快对抗样本生成的一条简单且通用的途径。

Abstract: Generating adversarial examples at scale is a core primitive for robustness evaluation, adversarial training, and red-teaming, yet even "fast" attacks such as FGSM remain throughput-limited by the cost of a backward pass. We introduce a family of attacks that eliminates the backward pass by predicting the input gradient from forward-pass hidden states via a lightweight linear regression. The approach is motivated by a kernel view of neural networks and is exact in the Neural Tangent Kernel regime, while remaining effective for practical finite-width models. Empirically, our methods recover much of FGSM's attack performance while using only a small fraction of the time, corresponding to a $532\%$ increase in throughput. These results suggest gradient prediction as a simple and general route to significantly faster adversarial generation under realistic wall-clock constraints.

</details>


### [241] [AIMing for Standardised Explainability Evaluation in GNNs: A Framework and Case Study on Graph Kernel Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14884)
*Magdalena Proszewska,N. Siddharth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了AIM评估框架，用于全面衡量图神经网络的可解释性（包括准确性、实例级和模型级解释），并通过案例研究展示了如何利用评估洞察改进模型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络（GNN）的可解释性评估框架存在明显局限：主要关注事后解释，难以跨模型比较；针对内在可解释模型的评估不够全面，缺乏多维度洞察的生成能力。因此迫切需要一个灵活、全面的评估框架来推动可解释AI的发展。

Method: 提出了AIM框架，从三个维度评估可解释性：准确性（Accuracy）、实例级解释（Instance-level explanations）和模型级解释（Model-level explanations）。该框架设计约束最小化以保证灵活性。研究者将其应用于管道流程：从内在可解释模型（如图核网络GKNs和原型网络PNs）提取解释，使用AIM评估，识别局限并获取洞察。以GKN为案例，基于AIM洞察开发了改进模型xGKN。

Result: AIM框架成功应用于多种内在可解释GNN模型的评估，有效识别了现有模型的局限性。以GKN为例，通过AIM获得的洞察指导开发了改进模型xGKN，该模型在保持高准确性的同时，显著提升了可解释性表现。

Conclusion: AIM框架填补了GNN可解释性评估领域的空白，为理解和改进复杂模型提供了实用解决方案，有助于推动图神经网络可解释AI领域的发展。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have advanced significantly in handling graph-structured data, but a comprehensive framework for evaluating explainability remains lacking. Existing evaluation frameworks primarily involve post-hoc explanations, and operate in the setting where multiple methods generate a suite of explanations for a single model. This makes comparison of explanations across models difficult. Evaluation of inherently interpretable models often targets a specific aspect of interpretability relevant to the model, but remains underdeveloped in terms of generating insight across a suite of measures. We introduce AIM, a comprehensive framework that addresses these limitations by measuring Accuracy, Instance-level explanations, and Model-level explanations. AIM is formulated with minimal constraints to enhance flexibility and facilitate broad applicability. Here, we use AIM in a pipeline, extracting explanations from inherently interpretable GNNs such as graph kernel networks (GKNs) and prototype networks (PNs), evaluating these explanations with AIM, identifying their limitations and obtaining insights to their characteristics. Taking GKNs as a case study, we show how the insights obtained from AIM can be used to develop an updated model, xGKN, that maintains high accuracy while demonstrating improved explainability. Our approach aims to advance the field of Explainable AI (XAI) for GNNs, providing more robust and practical solutions for understanding and improving complex models.

</details>


### [242] [Critic-Driven Voronoi-Quantization for Distilling Deep RL Policies to Explainable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14897)
*Senne Deproost,Denis Steckelmacher,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Critic-Driven Voronoi State Partitioning (CD-VSP) 的新方法，通过利用评论家网络将深度强化学习策略蒸馏为多个线性子策略的组合，在保持性能的同时显著提升了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度强化学习策略蒸馏方法难以平衡性能与可解释性的权衡，且传统蒸馏仅最小化原始策略与代理策略之间的行为差异，忽略了动作价值等强化学习特有的组件。

Method: 提出Critic-Driven Voronoi State Partitioning方法，利用原始策略的评论家价值网络，迭代地在价值不足的区域引入新的子策略。该方法使用Voronoi量化器通过最近邻查找将状态空间划分为多个区域，每个区域用梯度下降优化一个线性函数。

Result: 在多个知名基准测试上验证了该方法的有效性，证明了这种蒸馏方法可以使用合理数量的线性函数逼近原始策略的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了深度强化学习策略的可解释性问题，通过将复杂策略分解为多个简单的线性子策略，实现了性能与可解释性之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: Despite many successful attempts at explaining Deep Reinforcement Learning policies using distillation, it remains difficult to balance the performance-interpretability trade-off and select a fitting surrogate model. In addition to this, traditional distillation only minimizes the distance between the behavior of the original and the surrogate policy while other RL-specific components such as action value are disregarded. To solve this, we introduce a new model-agnostic method called Critic-Driven Voronoi State Partitioning, which partitions a black box control policy into regions where a simple class of model can be optimized using gradient descent. By exploiting the critic value network of the original policy, we iteratively introduce new subpolicies in regions with insufficient value, standing in for a measure of policy complexity. The partitioning, a Voronoi quantizer, uses nearest neighbor lookups to assign a linear function to each point in the state space resulting in a cell-like diagram. We validate our approach on several well known benchmarks and proof that this distillation approaches the original policy using a reasonable sized set of linear functions.

</details>


### [243] [TILBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Tabular Imbalanced Learning Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14915)
*Ruizhe Liu,Jiaqi Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TILBench基准，在57个表格数据集上系统评估了40多种不平衡学习方法，发现没有单一方法在所有设置中均占优，方法的有效性依赖于数据集特征和计算约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不平衡学习在表格数据应用中仍是基本挑战，尽管已有大量研究，但缺乏对不同不平衡学习方法在多样化数据特征下的系统实证理解，特别是不同方法族在预测性能、鲁棒性和计算可扩展性方面的比较仍不清楚。

Method: 构建了大规模表格不平衡学习基准TILBench，在57个多样化表格数据集上评估了40多种代表性算法，通过超过200,000个受控实验，全面分析了不同数据特征下各方法的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，没有单一方法在所有设置中始终占优；不平衡学习方法的有效性强烈依赖于数据集特征和计算约束，不同方法族在不同条件下表现各异。

Conclusion: 基于实验发现，本文为实际应用中选择合适的不平衡学习方法提供了实用建议，强调了根据数据集特征和计算约束进行方法选择的重要性。

Abstract: Imbalanced learning remains a fundamental challenge in tabular data applications. Despite decades of research and numerous proposed algorithms, a systematic empirical understanding of how different imbalanced learning methods behave across diverse data characteristics is still lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how different method families compare in predictive performance, robustness under varying data characteristics, and computational scalability. In this work, we present Tabular Imbalanced Learning Benchmark (TILBench), a large-scale empirical benchmark for tabular imbalanced learning. TILBench evaluates more than 40 representative algorithms across 57 diverse tabular datasets, resulting in over 200000 controlled experiments across a wide range of data characteristics. Our findings show that no single method consistently dominates across all settings; instead, the effectiveness of imbalanced learning methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics and computational constraints. Based on these findings, we provide practical recommendations for selecting appropriate methods in real-world applications.

</details>


### [244] [A Mutual Information Lower Bound for Multimodal Regression Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14917)
*Leonardo Ferreira Guilhoto,Akshat Kaushal,Paris Perdikaris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MI-LB采集函数，通过双指标框架分解认知不确定性与偶然不确定性，解决了连续回归中多模态预测分布下的主动学习难题，在多模态基准测试中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的连续回归主动学习方法在预测分布为多模态时存在缺陷：方差无法捕捉模态间的分歧，而BALD等信息论目标是为离散输出设计的，缺乏针对连续输出且能分离认知不确定性的采集函数。

Method: 引入双指标框架：一个随机索引在竞争模型假设间选择（认知不确定性来源），第二个索引控制假设内随机性（偶然不确定性来源）。通过熵分解，识别输出与认知索引间的互信息作为采集目标，并针对连续输出的不可处理性，推导出互信息下界（MI-LB）采集函数，为混合密度网络集成提供闭式近似。

Result: 证明了该互信息随训练数据增加而趋近于零，确认其捕捉了数据可解决的不确定性。在多模态系统基准测试中，MI-LB匹配或超越所有基线方法，且是唯一表现一致的方法；几何和Fisher基线方法仅在输入空间已编码多模态时才具竞争力，否则会失效。

Conclusion: MI-LB采集函数成功解决了连续回归中多模态预测分布下的主动学习问题，通过理论证明和实验验证，确立了其在捕捉认知不确定性方面的有效性和优越性，为该领域提供了原理性的解决方案。

Abstract: Active learning for continuous regression has lacked an acquisition function that targets epistemic uncertainty when the predictive distribution is multimodal: variance misses modal disagreement, and information-theoretic targets like BALD are designed for discrete outputs. We introduce a Two-Index framework that makes this separation explicit: one stochastic index selects among competing model hypotheses (epistemic source), while a second governs within-hypothesis randomness (aleatoric source). An entropy decomposition within the framework identifies the mutual information between the output and the epistemic index as a principled acquisition objective, and we prove this quantity vanishes as the model is trained on growing datasets, confirming that it captures exactly the uncertainty data can resolve. Because this mutual information is intractable for continuous outputs, we derive the Mutual Information Lower Bound (MI-LB) acquisition function, a closed-form approximation for Mixture Density Network ensembles. On benchmarks featuring multimodal systems, MI-LB matches or beats every baseline evaluated and is the only method to do so consistently -- geometric and Fisher-based baselines compete only when the input space already encodes the multimodality, and collapse otherwise.

</details>


### [245] [Learning with Shallow Neural Networks on Cluster-Structured Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14927)
*Elisabetta Cornacchia,Laurent Massoulié*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对输入数据（如图像、文本）存在空间相关性的特点，提出了一个可处理的模型，证明了在浅层神经网络上使用分层梯度下降学习时，样本复杂度仅与隐变量数量有关，在高信噪比下独立于输入维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的高维数据（图像、文本、基因序列等）在输入空间内部表现出强烈的空间相关性，而现有的深度学习理论模型通常假设低维结构仅存在于目标函数中（将非结构化输入投影到低维子空间）。这种假设与实际数据的特性不符，因此需要研究输入内部的相关性如何影响学习的样本复杂度。

Method: 作者提出了一个可处理的理论模型，其中目标函数依赖于少量潜在布尔变量，输入特征被分组为簇并与潜在变量相关联。在可识别性假设下，作者分析了分层梯度下降（layerwise gradient-descent）变体在浅层神经网络上的表现。

Result: 理论分析表明，该算法的样本复杂度与隐藏变量的数量成正比；当信噪比足够高时，样本复杂度独立于输入维度（仅相差对数项）。作者在合成数据和真实数据上进行了实验，验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了理论模型与现实数据特性之间的空白，证明了利用输入数据内部的相关性，梯度下降可以在高维设置下实现高效学习，其样本复杂度不受输入维度的显著影响，为理解深度学习在高维数据上的成功提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The success of deep learning in high-dimensional settings is often attributed to the presence of low-dimensional structure in real-world data. While standard theoretical models typically assume that this structure lies in the target function, projecting unstructured inputs onto a low-dimensional subspace, data such as images, text or genomic sequences exhibit strong spatial correlations within the input space itself. In this paper, we propose a tractable model to study how these correlations affect the sample complexity of learning with gradient descent on shallow neural networks. Specifically, we consider targets that depend on a small number of latent Boolean variables, and input features grouped into clusters and correlated with the latent variables. Under an identifiability assumption, we show that for a layerwise gradient-descent variant, the sample complexity scales with the number of hidden variables and, when the signal-to-noise ratio is sufficiently high, is independent of the input dimension, up to logarithmic terms. We empirically test our theoretical findings on both synthetic and real data.

</details>


### [246] [Slot-MPC: Goal-Conditioned Model Predictive Control with Object-Centric Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14937)
*Jonathan Spieler,Angel Villar-Corrales,Sven Behnke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Slot-MPC，一个结合以对象为中心的世界模型和模型预测控制(MPC)的框架，通过基于梯度的规划实现对新场景的适应，在机器人操作任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以对象为中心的世界模型和强化学习方法通常学习在推理时固定的反应式策略，限制了对新情况的泛化能力。作者希望克服这一局限，使智能体能够适应未见过的场景。

Method: Slot-MPC利用视觉编码器学习基于槽位的表示来编码场景中的单个对象，并使用这些结构化表示学习动作条件的以对象为中心的动力学模型。在推理时，利用学习到的动力学模型通过MPC进行动作规划。由于世界模型是可微的，可以使用基于梯度的MPC直接优化动作，比基于采样的无梯度MPC方法计算效率更高。

Result: 在模拟机器人操作任务上的实验表明，与非以对象为中心的世界模型基线相比，Slot-MPC提高了任务性能和规划效率。在有限状态-动作覆盖的离线设置中，基于梯度的MPC比基于采样的无梯度MPC表现更好。

Conclusion: 显式结构的以对象为中心的表示为可控和可泛化的决策提供了强大的归纳偏置。基于梯度的MPC在有模型预测控制需求且数据覆盖有限的场景下更具优势。

Abstract: Predictive world models enable agents to model scene dynamics and reason about the consequences of their actions. Inspired by human perception, object-centric world models capture scene dynamics using object-level representations, which can be used for downstream applications such as action planning. However, most object-centric world models and reinforcement learning (RL) approaches learn reactive policies that are fixed at inference time, limiting generalization to novel situations. We propose Slot-MPC, an object-centric world modeling framework that enables planning through Model Predictive Control (MPC). Slot-MPC leverages vision encoders to learn slot-based representations, which encode individual objects in the scene, and uses these structured representations to learn an action-conditioned object-centric dynamics model. At inference time, the learned dynamics model enables action planning via MPC, allowing agents to adapt to previously unseen situations. Since the learned world model is differentiable, we can use gradient-based MPC to directly optimize actions, which is computationally more efficient than relying on gradient-free, sampling-based MPC methods. Experiments on simulated robotic manipulation tasks show that Slot-MPC improves both task performance and planning efficiency compared to non-object-centric world model baselines. In the considered offline setting with limited state-action coverage, we find that gradient-based MPC performs better than gradient-free, sampling-based MPC. Our results demonstrate that explicitly structured, object-centric representations provide a strong inductive bias for controllable and generalizable decision-making.
